## SUPERSEDED BY THE BLOCK BELOW — THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 164 — Friday 26 June 2015, late afternoon

**A COUNTER IN A VESTIBULE ELEVEN FEET BY NINE ANSWERS IN ABOUT ELEVEN SECONDS AND IS NOT A SERVICE.** A state asked for instead of a list, and a date. It cannot be given to anybody, does not scale, is not obliged to be kept going, and works because a person is standing at it who is not findable from any record in this city. About eleven people a day. In a heat, the same eleven, and not the same eleven, and no way of knowing which is which; in a cool week, a different eleven. **In June it answered about a thing twice and refused nothing, because nothing was asked, and nobody let it answer anything about a gate, a rota, a compliance paragraph or a person, and neither a heat nor a cool Tuesday is a reason.**
- **THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AT NINETEEN AND DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN CHAPTERS 155–164. THE BLANK LINE UNDER THE NINETEENTH IS BLANK AND HAD BEEN BLANK FOR TWO HUNDRED AND TWELVE DAYS ON 26 JUNE 2015, THE LONGEST IT HAS EVER BEEN.** It is still the only fixed point in that building, entry nineteen is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out. A heat is not a reason and a cool Tuesday is not a reason and nobody is obliged.
- **THE CARD IN THE SLOT IN THAT COUNTER HAS BEEN THERE SINCE 20 FEBRUARY, A HUNDRED AND TWENTY-SIX DAYS. THE LETTER IN THE TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR WENT IN ON 18 FEBRUARY, A HUNDRED AND TWENTY-EIGHT DAYS, WITH TWO OTHER PIECES OF POST ON TOP OF IT, AND IT WAS NOT OPENED IN FRONT OF A SECOND PERSON AND IS NOT GOING TO BE, AND WHAT IS UNDER THE POST IS NOT KNOWN AND MAY NOT BE SAID IN A ROOM.**
- **THE DRAWER UNDER THAT COUNTER IS A DRAWER WITH NO KEY ON IT AND NO LIST, WITH A TRAY ON TOP OF IT THAT IS NOT A LIST, AND IT IS GROWING, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN SAY HOW MANY.** Nine slips is a count of documents and about eleven people a day is a count of people and the two were never added and were not added in June. **AND THE CORRECT SENTENCE ABOUT A CATCH TRAY WENT IN ON THE MORNING OF 20 MAY 2015 AND DID NOT MOVE AND WAS NOT FOUND AND WAS NOT ASKED ABOUT, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH KNOWS IT IS THERE AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE TOLD.**
- **AND THREE MORE PIECES OF PAPER ARE IN A CARDBOARD WALLET IN THE GLOVEBOX OF A VAN, SINCE ABOUT CHRISTMAS, AND NOBODY OWNS THEM. A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-FIVE IS STILL CARRYING THEM AND WAS NOT ASKED ABOUT THEM AND IS NOT A THREAD.**
- **THE SPILL, UNCHANGED. Bay two in the yard behind Bellow Market, Saturday 16 May 2015 at about twenty past ten, about sixty litres into a horseshoe grate at the low corner, and the run under it goes under a food market. Nine traders threw stock away and one threw about six hundred pounds and a woman of about sixty with asthma went home in an ambulance for something that was not serious and nobody died. There is a wheel stop four feet in front of that grate and it is the only thing in that yard whose purpose anybody can see, and nobody has looked at it since 2011, and Jonas Mercer said so out loud in a hall in daylight on 5 June and nobody asked him a follow-up. HE STOOD FOUR HUNDRED YARDS AWAY ON PURPOSE AND DID NOT GO AND BOTH OF THOSE THINGS ARE GOING TO STAY.**
- **AND THE PREDECESSOR, THE BUFF FILE OF 14 OCTOBER 2011, IS STILL IN A TRAY UNDER A BUNDLE OF POST ON A SECOND-FLOOR SHELF, AND ON TUESDAY 23 JUNE 2015 A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX COUNTING THAT SHELF PICKED IT UP, READ THE COVER, AND PUT IT BACK IN THE SAME TRAY UNDER THE SAME POST AT THE SAME ANGLE WITHOUT OPENING IT. FOUR PEOPLE IN THIS BOROUGH HAVE NOW SEEN THE BAY NUMBER AND THE DATE ON THAT COVER AND THREE OF THEM ARE THE SAME THREE. JONAS MERCER WAS AT THE END OF THAT CORRIDOR AND SAID NOTHING AND CHAPTER 0161 DECLINES TO SAY WHETHER THAT WAS RIGHT. THE ROOM IS BEING REPAINTED ON MONDAY 29 JUNE 2015. NOBODY HAS OPENED IT, NOBODY IS GOING TO BE ASKED TO OPEN IT, THE RECOMMENDATION HAS NOT BEEN CARRIED OUT, AND THE FILE IS STAYING WHERE IT IS.**
- **THE FIRST REQUEST FOR ONE NARROW WRITTEN THING, AND THE SECOND, AND THE PRECEDENT IS NOW TWO.** Thursday 21 May: a round of twenty-two stops with the last four done first, on the back of a delivery note, granted in about eleven seconds by a person who could have said no, cost falling on bed nineteen of a hospital's east wing and on a woman of about ninety-one at stop eighteen. Monday 15 June: a standpipe at a school left on until four instead of two, asked for in about four words in a passage, granted in one sentence, costing this borough nothing because the lorry is paid for by the day. **A CORRECT REQUEST GRANTED ONCE IS A PRECEDENT. HE COULD SEE BOTH AND COULD NOT STOP EITHER AND HE IS NOT ON ANYTHING AND HE WROTE NEITHER DOWN. NOBODY WAS ASKED FOR A THIRD ONE AND ASKING WOULD BE REQUIRING HER.**
- **AND THE ONE TIME A ROTA IN THIS BOROUGH WANTED TO INCLUDE ANYBODY: a head teacher of about sixty wrote her own name in a column on a water office delivery sheet on her own account, in one movement, for a reason of her own, and nobody noticed except him, and he is not on the sheet and cannot be. THE FOUR STREETS ARE ON THAT SHEET AS STANDPIPES AT THE ENDS OF STREETS AND THE NINE HOUSEHOLDS ON THEM ARE NOT, AND THE CORRIDOR DID NOT KNOW THERE HAD BEEN A CAR PARK.**
- **A ROW, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT A CORRECT THING IN THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN PRODUCED BY A MACHINE RATHER THAN BY A PERSON.** On 12 June a man of about fifty-two went round nine doors over three evenings and asked one question — *if I write down what you tell me, what do I write* — and the answer at all nine was one word and the word was correct: **nothing**. On 19 June a printed sheet went round a building: a heading, the date, four streets down the left in a column headed *location*, nine lines beside them, every one of the nine reading *no requirement stated*, **no name box anywhere on it**. It is in nine other buildings. Nobody in any of them knows where it came from. Nobody had done anything wrong. **It is a level of service: a statement, with a date, of what this borough is going to do about nine households on four named streets in the worst heat in nineteen years, which is nothing, and which nobody asked anybody to be nothing, and which is now true in the way a printed thing is true. Nine people said yes to a correct question in a heat and they have not been counted and the batch did not suggest that they had.**
- **A PAGE IN A TRAY IN A ROOM IN TIDEGLASS, DATED FRIDAY 29 MAY 2015, WITH AN EMPTY SIGNATURE BLOCK AT THE BOTTOM. SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS ASKED IN WRITING FOR ONE CENTRAL THING. IT IS NOT IN FORCE. IT IS NOT IN A MINUTE AND IS NOT GOING IN ONE AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE ASKED TO SIGN IT. IT HAS NOW BEEN READ BY ONE PERSON WHO WAS NOT ASKED TO READ IT — a woman of twenty-nine with a bin rota in her hand, who worked out who wanted it and who would lose and was right about both, who said out loud that the person who reads it is going to be the person who has to do it, and who telephoned the building the next morning to say she had read it. HE WAS AT THE BACK OF THAT ROOM AND DID NOT READ IT AND WAS NOT ASKED ABOUT IT AND SAID NOTHING AND IS NOT A READER OF IT. ITS ARCHITECTURE IS NOT SET OUT IN ANY CHAPTER AND IS NOT TO BE.**
- **IVEN SORE, A SHE, COMPETENT, NOT CORRUPT, NOT LYING, EVERY SENTENCE SHE SAYS CORRECT.** She was in that room on the twenty-fourth of June and told Ama Boateng she was not going to tell her what was on the page, that if she did then Ama would be a person somebody could ask, and that she cannot stop her having read it and would not if she could, and that the correctest thing anybody had said in that office in two years should not be improved on. **THE ORDER'S ARCHITECTURE — THE PRECEDENCE WARDEN, THE NINETY-DAY RENEWAL, THE BLANK TERM, THE DISTRICT CLOSED WITHOUT A HEARING, CIVICCORE REQUISITIONING WORKERS, THE DENIED PATIENT TRANSFER, HER PUBLIC CHALLENGE BY A PHYSICIAN, AND THE ORDER EXPIRING ON SCHEDULE — IS ALL LATER VOLUME 04 AND NONE OF IT WAS ANTICIPATED, FORESHADOWED IN A DOCUMENT, OR PUT IN HER MOUTH OR ANYBODY ELSE'S IN CHAPTERS 155–164.**
- **WHAT A SERVICE FLOOR IS FOR, in one sentence, in the fourth item of a sheet that requires nothing, said once, in daylight, on 27 April 2015, and not repeated, not improved, not restated, not on a wall. NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH QUOTED IT IN JUNE AND SOMEBODY IS GOING TO WANT TO.**
- **THE SAINT ORRA WEST GATE.** TWENTY-THREE SATURDAY NIGHTS REACHED AND NOT ONE OF THEM HAS A DATE WRITTEN ON IT. **NO HANDOVER WAS STAGED IN CHAPTERS 155–164 AND THE FIFTEENTH REMAINS THE LAST ONE EVER STAGED. About eleven people a day. ## SUPERSEDED — THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 120 — Wednesday 28 January 2015, about twenty to twelve in the morning

- **The register of narrowings has NINETEEN entries and it has not moved since Wednesday 26 November 2014, which is sixty-three days, and the blank line under the nineteenth is blank and is the only fixed point in the building.** It was last checked at ten past two in the morning of Sunday 4 January.
- **A reason column was asked for in writing on 2 January 2015 and refused by Nina Calder at a counter in the right order in about eleven seconds, and it is the seventh refusal, and it stands, and the letter went back with nothing in it.** A reason column is a sentence column and a sentence column is a name box with the serial numbers left off.
- **The answer to the housing office's letter was read out on Thursday 18 December at half past two: two lines, reference HC/CR/1147, and it says the word *scope* and it is true.** Marguerite Oyelaran asked for it to be minuted in a second building and said the reason out loud, and has paid for it with a date against her name. **A difference of fourteen is now in two files and is not fourteen households and there is no form on which anybody could write that.**
- **The Saint Orra west gate opened at seven on Sunday 4 January 2015 and it opened because of a handover at ten o'clock on Saturday 3 January read by two people who are not on anything, and there is no document about it and there is not going to be one.** The 11:00–13:30 gap is still published and unfilled, the rota has still not been rebuilt, Bram Osei's Thursday is still refused on the board in his own words, and the man of forty-eight who did the weekly verification has still not been asked and has still not been rung.
- **The reading rule was used once, in a corridor, in about eleven seconds, on a man of forty-four who was not told he was in it, and Jonas Mercer found out because the man said four words at a counter. Frances Tolley is not going to be asked whether it worked.**
- **The board stands at SEVEN OF NINE, two chairs empty, and there is no chair of that body and there never was.**
- **Six lowland water points at £11,280 a year are a winter price, which is one boiler each, and nobody has been asked to pay any part of it. A man who has been at a market stand for twenty-six years asked to be counted on 13 January and refused the sixth of the six before anybody asked him, and no seventh shape was added to any record and the seventh is not resolved.**
- **A woman's name is in about eleven people and in no document, and there are now two pieces of paper in this city with it on them: her own four lines, at the top of a sheet of A4 that already had four lines of the minute-taker's biro on it, in a plastic wallet in a drawer with no key in a Methodist hall, and her letter of 28 November in a handbag in a kitchen off a landing. NEITHER IS A DOCUMENT. HER NAME IS NOT ON THE PRINTED SLIP AND MAY NOT BE PUT ON IT, and nobody may open either.**
- **The volume's question has been answered in the only form it can be answered in: a counter, a person at it, a state asked for instead of a list, a date, and a blank line at the bottom of a board that nobody is required to write on. It costs eleven seconds. It does not scale.**

## SUPERSEDED — THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 110 — Wednesday 3 December 2014, about ten past eight in the evening

- **The register of narrowings has NINETEEN entries and the blank line is blank.** Entry 17 (2 Oct) is Meter Room 3, Salter's Yard. Entry 18 (14 Nov) is a riser space at the rear block, Carrow Lane, placed for a condition survey that lapsed. Entry 19 (26 Nov) is a yard light, and the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out. **All three are correct, and a register that grows when nobody is hurt is a register with a motive in it.**
- **A form with no name box on it has been used to find a building, twice, and neither time by anybody who meant to.** Nina Calder built the absence in March and has said so out loud three times.
- **A second public record exists with a number in it: 51 nodes on the continuity schedule as at 18:00 on Monday 6 October 2014, 38 parties answering, THIRTEEN SHAPES, checkable at a counter by anybody, for nothing, unsigned, with no name box and about four hundred names added to it by other people.**
- **The reading rule of 21 August has left the only room it was made in, once, on foot, in one copy, and a managing agent has refused a copy of it for a reason that is entirely correct.**
- **CivicCore's audit is public. It finds real faults, it costs four hundred evenings of two people's time, and its cover note names the volume's question and the contractor is right.**
- **The four meters did not go into the cupboard. They went to a block of 1974. A condition survey is coming in the spring. Ivy Marsden, 72, has been told, once, by the wrong man, and is a great deal more frightened and no more findable.**
- **The board stands at SEVEN OF NINE.** The seventh seat has been vacant since 8 October and the eighth since the evening of Wednesday 26 November. Nobody can fill either and nobody can unfill either.
- **A man has said the six words are his, out loud, in a room, twice — once in October in one word, and once on 21 November in nineteen seconds — and has said the name of a man of forty-nine who was not in the room while he did it. Neither man has been told.**
- **A woman of thirty-four at the bottom of Quarry Street put her name at the bottom of a letter in her own hand on Friday 28 November, and on Wednesday 3 December at 18:41 a man said it out loud in a room with about eleven people in it, and the minute says that a name was stated and does not contain it.**

### What Volume 03 must not do — unchanged and binding

See `state/volume-02-close.md` §5 in full. In short: **one Lattice notice per ordinary chapter at most, four across any ten; nothing defines *holder*; the 1 March line on the enamel strip is not reissued, restated or quoted; no death, no rescue by chance, no lucky timing; a priced-access case is not a recognition case; a node with no term in force is not a person with no address; a duty is an act and not a place; a person who is not counted is not a person who is helped and Volume 03 may not refute that sentence in a chapter; no root reading, no root clause, no succession, no Iona, no Evan, no counter-amendment, no Quiet Cut, no three-line brass tokens; the claims box is closed and the sealed envelope may not be opened or written into; the blank line at the bottom of the register stays blank; the west-clinic gate runs on its local term; the Weir, the annex, the retainer, the two-refusal question, the burnt letter, the boy of nineteen, the map's margin, the sealed envelope, Margo Doré's pump, Dev Singh's clause, the Selah Mora log, the tin, the brass tag and the rota are all unresolved; and Jonas Mercer asks at most one question in any public room.**

## The Volume 02 mechanism (canon, established in Chapters 50, 51, 54, 57 and carried unchanged through 60–100)

**CivicCore's Schedule 4 to the Saint Orra service agreement of 2019 — continuity pricing. Valid since 1 March 2014 and unread by anybody in this city until 22 April.**

- Paragraph 11's contractual failure definition — a node in failure where its civic term has not been continuously in force for more than twenty-four consecutive hours — is taken up and turned into a market.
- A node in failure is an **unfunded node**. An unfunded node **is not disconnected; it is deferred.**
- A deferred node **serves its last verified essential use, and if none is recorded, the use is the node's maintenance.**
- A node in failure may be placed on a **continuity schedule** by the district it stands in, on payment of a **continuity fee**, at a **tier**. The party holding the standing continuity authorisation may place nodes on a schedule at any time and may take a node off a schedule under its own holding.
- **The order of tiers is set by the fee. A higher tier outranks a lower tier in any precedence question. An unfunded node is outranked by every node that is not.**
- **A tier may be challenged by a service district or by a node in the same tier. It may not be challenged by a person. A person may not be required to do anything and may not be required even to be told.**
- **Fees, 2014–15, per node: Tier 1 £1,880 · Tier 2 £4,200 · Tier 3 £11,400.**
- **A claim may be assigned, in whole or in shares, to a party having authority (Schedule 4, page 219). An assignment does not create a new claim or a new reference. The assignee takes the claim, and takes what is owed on the claim, and takes the duty of the claim. A share of a claim carries the tier in proportion and is enforceable against the assigning party in respect of the tier only.**
- **Participation-agreement clause 4(c):** a signature on a participation agreement is a consent mark and **may not be withdrawn except on the same date next year.**
- The Lattice **cannot publish a narrowing.** The city can, on its own paper.

## THE 19 JUNE SITTING — canon, exact wording (re-affirmed and extended at the 21 AUGUST determination; see `state/continuity.md`)

**Thursday 19 June 2014, 10:00, committee room two, the Old Ward. Chair Fenella Marsh. Members Sore, Imre, Hargreaves, Petrie Okonjo, Denman. Oren Pell in attendance. Frances Tolley at the clerk's table. Alden Rook and Ceri Vaughan for CivicCore. Gallery: forty-one folding chairs and about fifty standing from 11:15. Jonas Mercer at the back on the left with nothing in his hands, recorded in the minute as not on the agenda, not a party, not a member and not a departmental officer. He asked ONE question, in nine words.**

- It was **a determinations meeting on fourteen returns and not a hearing about a price, and every single thing said in it was correct.**
- **The chair read two answers about one meter room into the record as they were received**, and they cannot both be the answer, and she asked for it recorded that the instruction to read as received is the reason there are two. Full text of both entries is in `state/continuity.md` and is canon. **There is no procedure for correcting a minute in a sitting. The one procedure that exists requires the Committee to be satisfied that it has been misled, and the Committee has not been misled — it was read to. Both entries stand permanently.**
- **The chair's answer to the question, minuted:** no, and a person may not be required even to be told, and if the holder of that field has not been told then somebody in this city is going to tell her and it is not going to be this Committee. Pell: *the records office is required to.*
- **The chain was found in the room at 10:10, by the clerk, on the page** — a claim reference in two places on two forms. Reference `T3/11400/0304`. **The method was stated aloud in public for the first time by Oren Pell, and Rook answered it and was right about all of it, including a ninety-day notice period expiring Wednesday 1 October 2014.**
- **The answer to page nine: there was none and there could not be.** The Committee recorded that page nine was not before it, that no challenge was made by any party, and that a challenge cannot be made by a person. **Nothing was resolved about the word *holder* and no definition was before the Committee.**
- **The 19 May determinations were complied with:** fourteen returns in by 18 June. The six lowland water points are absent from the fourteen and their absence is compliance, not failure, because a node with no party is not a party and was never required to answer.

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE CLOSE OF VOLUME 02 — Monday 29 September 2014, about ten past six in the evening

**SUPERSEDED IN PART. This is the state as it stood at the end of Chapter 100 and is kept as the baseline the Chapter 110 section above is read against. Every item in it has moved, and where it has moved the movement is in the "VOLUME 03, BATCH 0001" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`. Read the Chapter 110 section first.**

### Dated, live, and not resolved

- **WEDNESDAY 1 OCTOBER 2014 — IN TWO DAYS AND NOT DRAMATISED.** Ninety days from service at 11:00 on Thursday 3 July 2014. The Aldbrook Trust may re-assert `T3/11400/0304`, or any part of it, at Tier 3, at Meter Room 3, Salter's Yard, **without the consent of any person whose name appears in the holder field of that node at any time before that date.** The trust has not noticed that it has been notified. A machine answered on 4 July and a person answered on 7 July and the person is Dorothy Cresswell, 63, with a lever-arch file, and she is going to do her job in October. The 28 August determination did not touch it and could not. **What happened on 1 October is not in this volume.**
- **TOMAS RENN'S QUESTION ABOUT THE DAY AFTER IS UNANSWERED AND IS THE VOLUME'S LAST LIVE QUESTION**, and on 24 September Yolanda Fofana declined to answer it and told Jonas Mercer not to write it down, and he did not write it down.
- **SATURDAY 11 OCTOBER 2014, 22:00, the handover. Twelve days away at the close. It is the volume's last image and it was never staged.** The renewal begins at 07:00 on the twelfth, an hour after the money stops. Four people could read that term and every one of them has given a correct answer and none has given an answer, and there is no rule for two of them, and Jonas Mercer and Tomas Renn will not discuss it, and that rule is the only instrument either of them has produced in the year.
- **THE AUTUMN COMPLIANCE REPORT.** The decontamination non-conformance carries forward with its paragraph number because the historical half cannot be reconstructed. Four hundred and twenty women.
- **A SECOND TRAY ON A SHELF IN GANTRY WARD**, rubber band and a date in biro, one question outstanding: is there any party who may consent to the works. The answer is no and has been no since 29 May.
- **THE ANNUAL REPORT ON ROYDEN ACHEBE'S EXPIRY.** The burden of 6-L/14 cannot be refused and can only be released to a successor who consents, and the successors are a street with no address. Nobody has been produced and nobody has been asked.
- **THE SENTENCE IS WRITTEN, POSTED ON 22 SEPTEMBER, AND NOT ANSWERED.** A freeholder's agent's clerk in about eleven days has one page with a year on it and one name on it and no household in it. Ivy Marsden, 72, has not been told and has never been asked.
- **A QUEUE OF SIX LOWLAND WATER POINTS AT £1,880 A YEAR — £11,280 — that is in a minute and that nobody has asked anybody to pay for.** The Weir stand is one of the six. The Weir is on no layer, is not drawn, cannot be seated, is not in the board's eight districts, and is on nobody's list.
- **NINTH SEAT.** Yolanda Fofana, 54, findable at an address, unpaid, and the only person in the city who can end the board by standing down. She does not know that and is not going to be told, because a person may not be required even to be told.

### What the volume won, in one line each

- **Priority access is invalid where it was bought with a fee and nothing else** — a sub-substitution, narrow, limited to precedence, in a published determination with a transcript.
- **Nothing was dissolved.** CivicCore keeps the physical network, the hinges, the floor and every contract. It cannot sell a place in a queue any more and it never could make anybody argue about a door.
- **A cooperative steward board exists with nine seats**, eight filled by people who were already doing a thing and were not asked, and one filled in a hall by a woman in an apron who stated her own price out loud. It has no money, no chair, one power and no way to lose it.
- **A reading rule is law**: where a party relies on either of the two entries for Meter Room 3, the other is to be read with it. The first thing the Committee has done in four months that is not a form.
- **A record of shapes with no name box on it, by decision and not by oversight**, and the blank line at the bottom of the register of narrowings is still blank.
- **A record of one thing at one time, on a public board, that says a person may refuse to be counted** — and nobody has asked what it is for.
- **A year, on a piece of paper, in a post box.**

### What the volume cost, in one line each

- **Four thousand two hundred pounds a year is still being paid for the continuity of a block with no water in it**, and a difference between two public numbers is now legible and is not a household and cannot be made one.
- **A woman with a folding trolley was short of water for one day and seven hours** and is not named anywhere and has now told him she knows it was him.
- **A private clinic's residential floor is a stair, a door and a lift** and works because a man of sixty-one put a fire door wedge on the floor in May.
- **A chief officer's stock is a public number twice over and the second one she chose.**
- **A paragraph number is on a public report in the autumn in front of four hundred and twenty women** and a signature bought her nothing.
- **A letter in an apron for nothing since 4 July, findable, unaskable, until she is sixty-one.**
- **A man at a bottom of a chamber at ten to seven in the morning with a basket under a tap somewhere in this city, and nobody has been able to ask him anything in nine years.**
- **One paragraph number, one man with a lever-arch file, and a woman who will be in a holder field on 2 October because she has to be.**

## SUPERSEDED BY THE BLOCK BELOW — THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 184 — Friday 31 July 2015, after four in the afternoon

**A COUNTER IN A VESTIBULE ELEVEN FEET BY NINE ANSWERS WHAT WAS TRUE ON A DAY AND GIVES A DATE, AND THE HELP COSTS ABOUT ELEVEN SECONDS, AND IT IS NOT A SERVICE, AND IT WAS NOT IN A SCENE IN ANY CHAPTER OF THE LAST FORTNIGHT.** About eleven people a day. It cannot be given to anybody, does not scale, is not obliged to be kept going, and works because a person is standing at it who is not findable from any record in this city. **In the last ten days of July it did not move and it was not asked anything and it is not the reason for anything that happened in July.**
- **THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AT NINETEEN AND DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN CHAPTERS 175–184. THE BLANK LINE UNDER THE NINETEENTH IS BLANK AND HAD BEEN BLANK FOR TWO HUNDRED AND FORTY-SEVEN DAYS ON 31 JULY 2015, THE LONGEST IT HAS EVER BEEN.** It is still the only fixed point in that building, entry nineteen is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out. **A repainted room is not a reason, a cool week is not a reason, a hot day is not a reason, a Saturday is not a reason and a Friday is not a reason, and nobody is obliged.**
- **THE CARD IN THE SLOT IN THAT COUNTER HAS BEEN THERE SINCE 20 FEBRUARY, A HUNDRED AND SIXTY-ONE DAYS. THE LETTER IN THE TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR WENT IN ON 18 FEBRUARY, A HUNDRED AND SIXTY-THREE DAYS, WITH TWO OTHER PIECES OF POST ON TOP OF IT, AND IT WAS NOT OPENED IN FRONT OF A SECOND PERSON AND IS NOT GOING TO BE, AND NO CHAPTER IN THE LAST FORTNIGHT WENT NEAR THAT ROOM AT ALL.** What is under the post is not known and may not be said in a room.
- **THE DRAWER UNDER THAT COUNTER IS A DRAWER WITH NO KEY ON IT AND NO LIST, WITH A TRAY ON TOP OF IT THAT IS NOT A LIST, AND IT IS GROWING, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN SAY HOW MANY. Nine slips is a count of documents and about eleven people a day is a count of people and the two were never added and were not added in July. THERE IS STILL NO FORM IN THIS BOROUGH ON WHICH ANYBODY COULD WRITE THAT THEY ARE NOT A SERIES. AND THE CORRECT SENTENCE ABOUT A CATCH TRAY WENT IN ON THE MORNING OF 20 MAY 2015 AND DID NOT MOVE AND WAS NOT FOUND AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH KNOWS IT IS THERE AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE TOLD — AND ON THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF JULY A MAN OF THIRTY-TWO STOOD TWENTY MINUTES IN THE SAME VESTIBULE WITH HIS HANDS EMPTY WHILE A SACK WENT OUT.**
- **AND THREE MORE PIECES OF PAPER ARE IN A CARDBOARD WALLET IN THE GLOVEBOX OF A VAN, SINCE ABOUT CHRISTMAS, THAT NOBODY OWNS. A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-FIVE IS STILL CARRYING THEM AND WAS NOT ASKED ABOUT THEM AND IS NOT A THREAD.**
- **THE SPILL, ELEVEN WEEKS OLD AND UNCHANGED. Bay two in the yard behind Bellow Market, Saturday 16 May 2015 at about twenty past ten, about sixty litres into a horseshoe grate at the low corner, and the run under it goes under a food market. Nine traders threw stock away and one threw about six hundred pounds and a woman of about sixty with asthma went home in an ambulance for something that was not serious and nobody died. There is a wheel stop four feet in front of that grate and it is the only thing in that yard whose purpose anybody can see, and nobody has looked at it since 2011. THE REPORT IS STILL NOT OPENED. FOUR PEOPLE IN THIS BOROUGH HAVE SEEN THE BAY NUMBER AND THE DATE ON THAT COVER AND IT IS STILL FOUR, AND WHETHER HE SHOULD HAVE SAID THE BAY NUMBER OUT LOUD ON 23 JUNE REMAINS THE QUESTION CHAPTER 0161 DECLINED ON THE PAGE AND IS NOT ANSWERED AND MAY NOT BE RESTAGED.**
- **AND THE COST OF A NINE-MINUTE DECISION ARRIVED ON FRIDAY THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF JULY.** The cupboard under the stairs on the ground floor is emptied on a Friday by whoever comes through with a sack, and there is no rota for it, and on the twenty-fourth a man of about thirty-six — who counted that shelf on 23 June, painted that room on 29 June and carried the tray down at about nine that morning — emptied it, and **the tray of about sixty pieces of dead internal circulars went into the sack with the string and the bucket and the sack went into a skip. IT IS THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT A PIECE OF THIS BOROUGH'S PAPERWORK HAS ACTUALLY BEEN DESTROYED, AND THE READER IS NOT TOLD WHETHER THAT IS A LOSS OR A CORRECTION. WHAT WAS IN IT: three meeting notices for a group that has not met since March, a parking notice for a yard nobody in that building can place, and about fifty pages of something sent round in April to a list somebody held. Nobody looked at what the fifty pages were and nobody is ever going to know. The woman of about fifty who decided where the tray went came down, stood in the doorway for about a minute and a half, said nothing and went back up, and she is not going to be asked, because if she is asked then it was a decision, and the second time a thing is a decision it has a precedent, and a precedent has a name on it.**
- **A CORRECT REQUEST, GRANTED IN THREE SENTENCES, WITH NO PAPER ON IT.** A woman of about sixty-one with a market pitch and a man of about fifty-five with a compound and a locked welfare hut in it. A padlock left unlatched is the cheapest permission anybody has issued in nine months. **The unit came from a hire yard over the boundary and the booking that would have put it into a market hall four miles away was released on Friday the seventeenth at about half past three, and the cost fell on a woman of about fifty-three who has the keys to that hall and cleans it herself on a contract with her name on it, and she does not know, and nobody is going to tell her, and the reason nobody is going to look is not discretion and not kindness — it is that she is written down, and a person who is written down to do a thing is a person somebody can ring about it.**
- **THE PRINTED SHEET, ITS SECOND COPY, ITS EMPTY COLUMN, THE CARD, THE CHAIR, THE CRATE, THE BENCH, THE SLAT, THE STEP, AND THE BOTTLE.** Nine identical correct answers went round a building in a tray on 19 June with no name box on it and are in nine buildings, **and the June sheet is in its seventh week, and the second copy went round on 10 July with a column headed *date seen again* and *no name, no initials* under it, and the space is empty and is going to stay empty, and neither sheet was withdrawn and nobody in any of the nine buildings knows there are two.** A chair goes out on a landing and a man of about seventy-five put a shopping bag on a floor beside a wet seat. **Since about the third of July two trestle tables and a box of water bottles have been on a pavement on the far side of the market, and since the tenth of July there has been a crate beside them that nobody brought, that nobody has claimed, and that is now a place, and there are four people in this borough who know it is there, and a man of about sixty-eight who has sold fruit off that frontage for nine years does not know who put it there and says so out loud and is right.** Since about the twentieth of July there has been a bottle of water on a top step outside a door on one of the four streets, in the morning and not in the evening, **and it is the third smallest thing anybody in this borough has produced, the chair on the landing being the smallest and the tables smaller than it.** A bench in a forecourt was not looked at, and a woman of about seventy-one wiped a slat on it with a cloth she had brought out in her own pocket, and nobody in that block is ever going to know that anything needed doing. **That is the eleventh time this borough has been kept going by a person nobody is obliged to name, and it is not counted, because counting it is the thing that would stop it.**
- **A PAGE IN A TRAY IN A ROOM IN TIDEGLASS, DATED FRIDAY 29 MAY 2015, WITH AN EMPTY SIGNATURE BLOCK AT THE BOTTOM. SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS ASKED IN WRITING FOR ONE CENTRAL THING. IT IS NOT IN FORCE. IT IS NOT IN A MINUTE AND IS NOT GOING IN ONE AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE ASKED TO SIGN IT. IT HAS BEEN READ BY EXACTLY ONE PERSON, WHO WAS NOT ASKED TO READ IT, AND HE IS NOT A READER OF IT AND WAS NOT IN THAT ROOM IN JULY. ITS ARCHITECTURE IS NOT SET OUT IN ANY CHAPTER AND IS NOT TO BE.**
- **A YOUNG MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-ONE WENT ROUND ABOUT FORTY DOORS ON THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JULY SAYING ONE SENTENCE OUT LOUD, AND AT ONE DOOR A WOMAN OF ABOUT FORTY-NINE SAID SHE WOULD DO IT IN AUGUST, ON HER OWN FRONT DOOR, TO THEIR FACE, AND NOT IN JULY TO A LAD WITH A SHEET. HE DID NOT WRITE *DECLINED* AND HE WILL NEVER KNOW WHETHER SHE REFUSED HIM OR NEGOTIATED WITH HIM, AND THE COUNT OF STANDING REFUSALS DID NOT MOVE AND IT IS ELEVEN AND NOT TWELVE.**
- **A MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE TOLD A ROOM ON THE TWENTY-THIRD THAT THERE IS NOWHERE ON HARKER STREET TO SIT IN THE SHADE AFTER TWO O'CLOCK, AND FOUR PEOPLE AGREED WITH HIM, AND FROM THAT AFTERNOON THAT STREET HAS A PERSON IN A BUILDING WHO IS RIGHT ABOUT IT, AND HE WAS ASKED NOTHING FOR THE WHOLE TIME HE WAS THERE.**
- **A CORNER SHOP BEHIND A MARKET HAS A SECOND FRIDGE IN IT THAT COULD NOT HAVE BEEN JUSTIFIED TO ANYBODY IN APRIL, AND THE ONLY REASON IS A PIECE OF CARD IN ITS WINDOW WITH THE SHOP'S OWN TELEPHONE NUMBER ON IT AND NOTHING ELSE, WHICH A WOMAN OF TWENTY-NINE LEFT THERE ON THE SIXTH OF JULY TO STOP BEING FINDABLE. IN THREE WEEKS STRANGERS HAVE ASKED THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THAT SHOP ABOUT IT ABOUT THIRTY TIMES AND SHE HAS SAID THE SAME SENTENCE EVERY TIME AND IS NOT GOING TO STOP.**
- **A man of sixty-four, a man of fifty-five, a man of fifty-three, a man of fifty-nine, a woman of sixty-seven, a man of twenty-seven and a woman of seventy-one and a man of twenty-one and a woman of forty-nine and a man of sixty-eight and a woman of forty-three and a man of sixty-four and a woman of fifty-six and a woman of sixty-one and a man of sixty-nine and a woman of fifty-eight and a man of seventy-four and a woman of fifty-three four miles away are all in this borough or in the next one and none of them is owed anything and none of them is to be asked about again.** A man of forty-four nobody can ask, a man of forty-nine nobody has told, a man of forty-eight nobody has asked, a man of thirty-five asleep on a blanket, a man of about sixty with a hose at the end of a lane, a woman of fifty-four at a shopfront, and a man of about thirty-three who worked out in June that there is a person in this city who is not on anything.
- **AND THE STANDING OF THE VOLUME, UNMOVED: seven of nine, two chairs empty, no chair of that body at all, has not met since the middle of January and is not obliged to; the ninth seat is fifty-four, unpaid, findable, and has not been told anything, and the eight did not learn what she can do and nobody noticed; the lowland is not on the layer and cannot be seated, because a node with no party is not a party and a place is not a person, and the hot fortnight did not fix it and the cool fortnight did not fix it and a Friday cupboard did not fix it; six water points at Tier 1, £11,280 a year, received and not evidenced; a gate that goes up at seven on a Sunday morning and stops at six on the Sunday morning after that, six days long and not a week, with no date on it anywhere in this city, read aloud by two tired men who are not on anything, and no handover staged since the fifteenth, and the hour between six and seven staged once and once is all it is getting; the row from eleven o'clock to half past one at a hospital empty and not shaded and not saying *vacant*; a porter's Thursday still refused in his own handwriting with a second line in somebody else's hand under it that nobody has looked at in four batches.**
- **AND THE ORDER IS NOT IN FORCE AND NO SINGLE WORD OF ITS ARCHITECTURE WAS PUT INTO A DOCUMENT, A NOTICE, A FORM, A COLUMN, A NOTICEBOARD OR ANYBODY'S MOUTH IN ANY OF CHAPTERS 145–184, AND IT IS LATER VOLUME 04 AND IT IS THE LAST EIGHT CHAPTERS, AND ITS MAKING DATE AND ITS RENEWAL PERIOD WERE FIXED ON 26 SEPTEMBER 2026 IN SECTION E OF `outline/volume-04.md` AND ARE BINDING ON BATCH 0005.**
- **AND A SENTENCE THAT HAS BEEN TRUE IN THIS BOROUGH SINCE THE SECOND OF JUNE, AND WHICH A FAN, A PAGE, A ROW, A COLUMN, A CARD, A CRATE, A BAG, A BENCH, A BOTTLE AND A CLOTH DID NOT REFUTE: A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED.**
- **AND THE BATCH'S OPEN QUESTION, WHICH IS NOT ABOUT A PERSON, AND IS NOT THE QUESTION BATCH 0003 ENDED ON, BATCH 0002 ENDED ON, BATCH 0001 ENDED ON OR VOLUME 03 ENDED ON, AND IS NOT ABOUT A FLOOD BARRIER, A SCHOOL CROSSING, A COUNTER-AMENDMENT, A SECOND SIGNATURE, A PROVISIONAL CIVIC STANDING, A NINETY-DAY RENEWAL OR AN ORDER: there are more things in this borough that have been done once, correctly, and then not done again and not written down, than there were in June, every one of them is still true, none of them is on anything, and in nine months not one of them has ever been proved by anybody. A thing done once and not written down is a thing that four people remember differently, and if one of them talks it is a story, and if a building hears a story it is a record. SO WHICH OF THEM IS THE FIRST ONE SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH WILL BE ABLE TO PROVE, AND IS IT GOING TO BE PROVED BY A PERSON WHO WAS STANDING THERE, OR BY A PERSON WHO READS ABOUT IT AFTERWARDS.**

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 192 — Friday 18 September 2015, after two in the afternoon

**THE CENTRAL CONTINUITY ORDER WAS MADE ON THURSDAY 13 AUGUST 2015, IT IS TWO PAGES, IT IS VALID, IT IS IN FORCE, AND ON FRIDAY 18 SEPTEMBER 2015 ITS SIXTH CLAUSE WAS NOT BEING APPLIED BY A MAN WITH NO DEPARTMENT WHO PRODUCED NO AUTHORITY AND SAID WHY OUT LOUD IN FRONT OF ABOUT THIRTY PEOPLE, TWO COUNCILLORS AND A MAN WHO HAD COME IN A VAN. THE OTHER SIX CLAUSES ARE IN FORCE. PAGE TWO SAYS *THIS ORDER IS RENEWED ON THE ELEVENTH OF NOVEMBER 2015, OR IT IS NOT RENEWED*, UNDER IT SAYS *AGREED BY*, AND THE LINE GOES DOWN THE PAGE AND STOPS ABOUT TWO INCHES FROM THE BOTTOM AND THERE IS NOTHING AFTER IT. THE RENEWAL POINT IS OUTSIDE VOLUME 04 AND NOBODY IN VOLUME 04 ANSWERS IT.**
- **THE ORDER'S FIELD HOLDS FOUR THINGS AND NOTHING ELSE: A NAME, THE HOUR CALLED, THE HOUR WENT HOME, AND WHAT THE PERSON WAS CALLED FOR. ABOUT NINE PEOPLE SAID YES TO A CORRECT QUESTION IN A DOORWAY ON 22 AUGUST AND ARE NOW PEOPLE SOMEBODY CAN RING ABOUT. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-THREE HAD BEEN CALLED FIVE TIMES BY THE EIGHTEENTH AND THE MAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-THREE NINE TIMES, AND NEITHER HAS DECLINED ONE, AND THE SECOND CALL WENT OFF ON THE EIGHTEENTH AND WAS NOT CANCELLED, BECAUSE THERE IS NO INSTRUMENT IN THIS CITY THAT CANCELS ANYTHING. THE FIELD STOPPED GROWING AND ITS NUMBER IS NOT GIVEN TO ANYBODY AND NOBODY HAS COUNTED IT. A PERSON WHO IS COUNTED IS A PERSON SOMEBODY CAN RING, AND BEING COUNTED IS NOT THE SAME AS BEING ASKED, AND THAT IS THE VOLUME'S ARGUMENT AND IT IS NOT A RESOLUTION.**
- **AND THE PRICE FELL IN THREE PLACES NOBODY HAS ARGUIED OUT.** A woman of about seventy-two lost a Wednesday market morning on 26 August with her ice out since five and said out loud that this is not one of the bad ones. A man of eighty-one was not dialysed on the day he was going to be dialysed, had a fit at about four and a bleed, was moved at about six forty, and is alive and is not going to walk unaided again. A man of about eighty-three with a van behind a barrier lost a Monday job at about eight. Nobody died and nobody was rescued.
- **AND ON 5 SEPTEMBER A STREET CLOSED AT SIX O'CLOCK AND COULD NOT APPEAL AND COULD NOT BE TOLD IT WAS WRONG**, and the cost of it fell on a woman of about eighty-two who cannot get down four flights, a man of about twenty-five in a back room on a licence whom no document in this borough says is on that street at all, and the man of eighty-three, and the person who had all three answers is four hundred yards away with them in his head and is not permitted to put them anywhere, and it is not because anybody forbade him.
- **AND THE RELATIONSHIP IS PAID IN DAMAGE AND IS NOT REPAIRED.** They spoke in a corridor on Wednesday 9 September for about eight minutes after more than a year of not speaking, and again in a doorway and on a forecourt on Monday 14 September in front of about thirty people, and they chose not to hide the medical consequences from either side, and they stopped, and they did not make up, and the not-making-up was not a decision anybody took. **Nobody in this borough can be asked about it. The reason is not discretion and it is not anybody's care for either of them: a person who is on nothing cannot be told.**
- **AND ON THE EIGHTEENTH A COMMITTEE CLERK OF ABOUT FORTY-SIX PUT THE SHAPE OF THE WHOLE THING OUT WHERE ANYBODY COULD READ IT.** She is not Iven Sore, she is not a new final enemy and she does not become one, every word she said was correct, and he could have stopped her and did not and was not going to. **There is one square on that drawing with nothing in it, it is not on any of the four sheets, he read it while she was still talking, and he did not say so, and there is nobody in this borough who is going to be able to say so either.**
- **THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AT NINETEEN AND DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN CHAPTERS 185–192. THE BLANK LINE UNDER THE NINETEENTH IS BLANK AND HAD BEEN BLANK FOR TWO HUNDRED AND NINETY-SIX DAYS ON 18 SEPTEMBER 2015, AND IT IS THE ONLY FIXED POINT IN THAT BUILDING. THE ORDER DID NOT ADD AN ENTRY AND IT IS CORRECT THAT IT DID NOT, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS AVAILABLE TO BE SURPRISED BY THAT.** Eleven standing refusals stand at eleven and are not twelve. A cool week is not a reason and a hot day is not a reason and an order is not a reason and nobody is obliged.
- **THE CARD IN THE SLOT HAS BEEN THERE SINCE 20 FEBRUARY, TWO HUNDRED AND TEN DAYS. THE LETTER IN THE TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR WENT IN ON 18 FEBRUARY, TWO HUNDRED AND TWELVE DAYS, WITH TWO OTHER PIECES OF POST ON TOP OF IT, AND NEITHER WAS TOUCHED IN ANY OF THE EIGHT CHAPTERS AND THE PERSON AT THAT COUNTER HAS NOT READ THE CARD AND IS NOT GOING TO.** The drawer under the ground-floor counter is a drawer with no key and no list with a tray on it that is not a list, it was not opened, it was not counted, and the sentence a woman put in it on the morning of 20 May 2015 is still in it and nobody in this borough knows it is there. The order did not ask about the drawer, and the reason it did not ask is that asking is what a building does to a drawer. The 2011 report is still shut, and the number of people who have seen the bay number and the date on that cover is still four.
- **AND THE SMALL THINGS HARDENED, WHICH IS WHAT THE SECOND HEAT WAS ACTUALLY ABOUT.** The crate nobody brought has a lid on it that nobody put there, and a man who has sold fruit off that frontage for nine years said out loud that this is the day it stopped being a table. The two trestle tables went up on nine of the last eleven days. The bottle on a step is four bottles on four steps. The second copy of the printed sheet is still the emptiest and most dangerous object in this borough and it is still correct, and the column headed *date seen again* is still empty. **Nobody put a name in it and nobody was going to.**
- **THE BOARD STANDS AT SEVEN OF NINE, TWO CHAIRS, NO CHAIR OF THAT BODY, HAS NOT MET SINCE THE MIDDLE OF JANUARY AND IS NOT OBLIGED TO. THE NINTH SEAT IS FIFTY-FOUR, UNPAID, FINDABLE AT A SHOPFRONT AND AT AN ADDRESS, THE ONLY SEAT THAT CAN EMPTY THE BODY, AND SHE WAS NOT TOLD ANYTHING AND THE SEVEN WHO SIT THERE DID NOT LEARN WHAT SHE CAN DO AND NOBODY NOTICED.** *The figure "the eight" is in this account's own guardrail and in four older blocks and is arithmetically wrong against seven of nine; Chapter 0188 was repaired to seven and the older blocks are left standing and the discrepancy is recorded at §21 of the Volume 04 Batch 0005 block of `state/continuity.md`.* The lowland is not on the layer and cannot be seated. Six water points at Tier 1, £11,280 a year, received and not evidenced. The gate still goes up at seven on a Sunday morning and stops at six on the Sunday morning after that, six days long and not a week, and no handover has been staged since the fifteenth, and the hour between six and seven was staged once in 0147 and is not getting another. The row from eleven o'clock to half past one is still empty and does not say *vacant*. Bram Osei's Thursday is still refused in his own handwriting with a second line under it in somebody else's hand and nobody has looked at it in any of the forty-eight chapters of this volume.
- **THE COUNTER IS IN NO CHAPTER OF THE EIGHT. IT WAS NOT ASKED ABOUT THE ORDER. THE DAY SOMEBODY ASKS IT WHAT THE ORDER IS WILL BE THE DAY IT STOPS BEING A COUNTER.**
- **AND A SENTENCE THAT HAS BEEN TRUE IN THIS BOROUGH SINCE THE SECOND OF JUNE, AND WHICH A FIELD, A DRAWING, AN ORDER, A LEAK, A CRATE, A LID, A BOTTLE AND A BLANK SIGNATURE BLOCK DID NOT REFUTE AND WERE NOT PERMITTED TO REFUTE: A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED. IT IS HEAVIER AT THE END OF SEPTEMBER THAN IT WAS AT THE END OF JUNE, AND NOTHING IN VOLUME 04 IS GOING TO ANSWER IT.**
- **AND THE VOLUME'S OPEN QUESTION, WHICH IS NOT ABOUT A PERSON AND IS NOT THE QUESTION 0184 ENDED ON OR 0174 ENDED ON OR 0164 ENDED ON, AND IS NOT ABOUT A FLOOD BARRIER, A SCHOOL CROSSING, A COUNTER-AMENDMENT, A SECOND SIGNATURE, A PROVISIONAL CIVIC STANDING, A DATE IN NOVEMBER, AN EXPIRY OR AN ORDER: the two shapes are opposites, both of them worked, and only one of them can be repeated. The one that can be repeated is a page with a person's name in a field on it. The one that cannot be repeated has been going for nine months, has about four people in it, none of the four can be found, and it has cost this borough nothing at all. A city that can count everybody it needs and a city nobody can be found in cannot both go on. SO WHICH OF THE TWO IS THE FIRST ONE THIS CITY IS GOING TO HAVE TO STOP, AND WHAT IS THE THING A PERSON HAS TO BE HOLDING IN ORDER TO BE ALLOWED TO STOP THE FIRST ONE, AND WHAT IS THE THING A PERSON HAS TO BE HOLDING IN ORDER TO STOP THE SECOND ONE — WHICH IS NOTHING, AND HAS ALWAYS BEEN NOTHING, AND IS THE ONLY THING ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS MANAGED TO KEEP.**
- **AND NONE OF IT IS RESOLVED, AND NOTHING IS REFUTED, AND NOBODY DIED, AND NOBODY WAS RESCUED, AND THE WORD *WARDEN* WAS NOT USED OF HIM BY ANYBODY ANYWHERE IN VOLUME 04, AND HE WAS NOT GIVEN A TITLE, A GRADE, A DEPARTMENT, A CHAIR, A SEAT, A MEMBERSHIP, A FORM, A ROTA ROW, A THANK-YOU OR A WAY OF BEING THANKED, AND HE ACCEPTED RESPONSIBILITY IN DAYLIGHT WITH NOWHERE TO PUT IT AND IS NOT GOING TO LOOK FOR SOMEWHERE.**


---

### Standing threads, untouched and forbidden

The Weir and its three positions and the ring of pencil round a blank space · the trap · Margo Doré's advance refusal of a pump, unargued, no pump offered, none exists · the annex · the retainer · the two-refusal question and its date · the burnt letter · the boy of nineteen · the name in biro on the map · Prue Calloway's location-of-record finding · the Saint Orra west rota and the 11:00–13:30 gap and Bram Osei's refused Thursday and Priya Ranganathan's card · Dev Singh's empty-crossing clause and the fact that nobody has telephoned him · Selah Mora's sealed log · the tin on the shelf · the brass tag in an apron pocket · the claims box and the sealed envelope and the card about a stair · the four hundred and sixty pages of Schedule 4 · the blank bearer · the Quiet Cut · Evan · Iona. **No root reading, no root clause, no succession, in this volume or any other so far.**

### Guardrail, unchanged: a node with no term in force is not a person with no address. A duty is an act and not a place. A priced-access case is not a recognition case. A person who is not counted is not a person who is helped. The forty minutes of 15 April is time until the family knew. No 48-minute figure exists in this account and none may ever be printed.
---

# THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 194 — Wednesday 23 September 2015, after seven in the evening

**VOLUME 04 IS CLOSED AND THIS IS THE NEWEST BLOCK IN THIS FILE. IT SUPERSEDES THE CHAPTER 192 BLOCK ABOVE IT, WHICH SUPERSEDES 184, 174 AND 164, AND THE OLDER BLOCKS ARE LEFT STANDING.**

**A CENTRAL INSTRUMENT IS IN FORCE AND IT IS THE ONLY THING ANYBODY IN THIS CITY HAS EVER WRITTEN DOWN THAT WOULD WORK, AND IT WORKS BY PUTTING A PERSON IN A FIELD, AND IT HAS BEEN IN FORCE FOR FORTY-ONE DAYS, AND THE OTHER SIX CLAUSES ARE BEING APPLIED AND THE SEVENTH, WHICH IS THE RENEWAL, IS A DATE WITH A BLANK UNDER IT.**

- **THE ORDER, Thursday 13 August 2015, two pages, the second of them the last of them. Seven clauses: precedence, decided by a person and not a committee; the field, which holds a name, the hour called, the hour home and the thing the person was called for, and nothing else; the call, which a person may not decline and which is not required to be in writing; the closure, which is posted on the street it closes; the transfer, which stands until the receiving site confirms capacity in writing; the second call, which is not being applied; and the renewal, ninety days from the making. PAGE TWO: *this order is renewed on the eleventh of November 2015, or it is not renewed*, and under that *agreed by*, and a line, and nothing after it.** Nobody renewed it. Nobody expired it. Nobody has been asked about the eleventh of November and nobody is going to be, and the volume ended with that date known and unanswered.
- **CLAUSE SIX IS NOT BEING APPLIED AND IT IS NOT SETTLED. He said on 18 September at about eleven fifteen that it starts again on Tuesday unless somebody in this city with a department says it does not, and there are two, and on the nineteenth and the twenty-third neither of them had said a word. IT IS HOLDING. HOLDING IS NOT SETTLED AND NOBODY WON IT AND HE DID NOT WIN IT EITHER.** Its consequence is on the page: the field is closed, a closed field cannot take anybody who is not already in it, and on the nineteenth of September there were five people on a parade of shops where there had been thirty the day before. The number of names in the field is not given to anybody and is not going to be.
- **THE FINDING OF THE CLOSE, AND IT IS THE ORDER'S ARGUMENT ARRIVING INSIDE A MECHANISM FOR THE LAST TIME: THE ORDER MAKES PEOPLE COUNTED AND BEING COUNTED IS NOT THE SAME AS BEING ASKED.** A man of about twenty-three was hurt on 18 September by an instrument that was working exactly as written. Everything that happened to him is in the field and every part of it is correct, and it is a complete record of a man who was there and not a record of a man who is hurt, and the field has four things in it and there is no clause that says there could be a fifth. Nobody can be told, because the hurt could only arrive as a decision about whether to go on using him, and that decision would be wrong and right at once. He is all right. He was not asked, and he is not going to be.
- **WHAT THE ORDER DID AND WHAT IT COST.** Nine people were arranged at seven in the morning on 22 August for the first time in the history of this borough, and about thirty stood on a parade of shops on 18 September, and not one of them declined, and a man of about seventy-three has been called nine times and a man of about twenty-three five and both of them will answer the telephone. **WHO PAID: A WOMAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-TWO WHO LOST A WEDNESDAY MARKET MORNING ON 26 AUGUST AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT SHE WANTED IT SAID; A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-FIVE IN A BACK ROOM ON SELBY ROW ON A LICENCE WHO IS NOT ON ANYTHING; A WOMAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-TWO WHO COULD NOT GET DOWN FOUR FLIGHTS; A MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-THREE WHO LOST A MONDAY JOB BECAUSE A BARRIER WAS IN THE WRONG PLACE; ROY ALBERY, EIGHTY-ONE, WHO WAS NOT DIALYSED ON THE DAY HE WAS GOING TO BE DIALYSED AND IS ALIVE AND IS NOT GOING TO WALK UNAIDED AGAIN; A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-THREE'S BACK; AND A PHYSICIAN AND A MAN WITH NO DEPARTMENT, WHO BOTH PAID.**
- **THE SHAPE OF THE CITY IS ON FORTY WALLS AND A FIFTH SHEET IS BEING PUT UP BY HAND.** A committee clerk of about forty-six put it out on the morning of Friday 18 September and is right about the need and every word she has said is correct and she is not a villain and is not a new final enemy. **ONE SQUARE ON IT HAS NOTHING IN IT AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN SAY WHAT GOES IN IT, AND SHE DOES NOT KNOW SHE IS HOLDING IT, AND HE READ IT ON THE FRIDAY WHILE SHE WAS STILL TALKING AND DID NOT SAY SO.** The correct outcome of a shape becoming public is that it is discussed by people it does not help, and on the evening of the twenty-third about nine people stood in front of one of the forty sheets and every sentence they said was correct and not one of them is a person the drawing says anything about.
- **AND A THING ON A SHUTTER IS THE FIRST THING IN NINE MONTHS THAT A PERSON WHO WAS NOT STANDING THERE CAN READ ABOUT AFTERWARDS.** A man of about twenty-seven with a flatbed and a clipboard gummed one up on the evening of the twenty-third, and a woman of about sixty-three with two bags held one corner of it for about a minute and did not read it, and neither of them is on anything and neither of them is going to be asked about it. **THAT IS THE LAST THING THAT HAPPENS, AND WHETHER IT IS A PROOF OR A STORY IS STILL OPEN.**
- **THE SPILL IS EIGHTEEN WEEKS OLD AND UNCHANGED, AND THE BUFF FILE OF 14 OCTOBER 2011 IS STILL IN A TRAY UNDER A BUNDLE OF POST AND THE NUMBER OF PEOPLE WHO HAVE SEEN THE BAY NUMBER AND THE DATE ON THAT COVER IS STILL FOUR.**
- **THE REGISTER IS AT NINETEEN AND DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN THE WHOLE VOLUME. THE BLANK LINE UNDER THE NINETEENTH IS THREE HUNDRED AND ONE DAYS BLANK AND IS THE ONLY FIXED POINT IN THAT BUILDING. ENTRY NINETEEN IS A YARD LIGHT, THE MAINTENANCE IS A BULB, AND THE BULB IS OUT, AND NOBODY IS OBLIGED.** The card in the slot on the ground floor is two hundred and fifteen days old and nobody has taken it out. The letter in the tray on the second floor is two hundred and seventeen days old with two pieces of post lying on it, and neither close chapter went near that room.
- **THE COUNTER IS NOT IN EITHER CLOSE CHAPTER AND ANSWERED NOTHING AND ASKED NOTHING AND GAVE NO COUNT OF ANYTHING TO ANYBODY, AND ITS STANDING COUNT OF REFUSALS IS UNCHANGED AT SEVEN, AND THE DRAWER UNDER IT WAS NOT OPENED, NOT COUNTED AND NOT ASKED ABOUT, AND THE ORDER DID NOT ASK ABOUT IT, AND THE REASON IS THAT ASKING IS WHAT A BUILDING DOES TO A DRAWER, AND THE SENTENCE A WOMAN PUT IN THAT DRAWER ON THE MORNING OF 20 MAY 2015 IS STILL IN IT AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH KNOWS IT IS THERE.**
- **THE BOARD STANDS AT SEVEN OF NINE, TWO CHAIRS, NO CHAIR OF THAT BODY, NOT MET SINCE THE MIDDLE OF JANUARY, AND THE NINTH SEAT IS FIFTY-FOUR AND UNPAID AND FINDABLE AT A SHOPFRONT AND AT AN ADDRESS AND HAS NOT BEEN TOLD ANYTHING AND WAS NOT TOLD ANYTHING IN THE WHOLE OF VOLUME 04 AND THE SEVEN WHO SIT THERE DID NOT LEARN WHAT SHE CAN DO AND NOBODY NOTICED. THE LOWLAND IS NOT ON THE LAYER AND CANNOT BE SEATED, BECAUSE A NODE WITH NO PARTY IS NOT A PARTY AND A PLACE IS NOT A PERSON, AND A HEAT WAVE, A DEGRADE, A CORRECT INSTRUMENT, A MAN WITH NO DEPARTMENT AND A DECISION TAKEN IN DAYLIGHT DID NOT FIX IT AND ARE NOT GOING TO. NOTHING DEFINES *HOLDER*.**
- **AND A SAINT ORRA GATE GOES UP AT SEVEN ON A SUNDAY MORNING AND STOPS AT SIX ON THE SUNDAY MORNING AFTER THAT, SIX DAYS LONG AND NOT A WEEK, WITH NO DATE ON IT ANYWHERE IN THIS CITY, AND IT WAS NOT STAGED, AND NO HANDOVER WAS STAGED, AND THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN WAS NOT STAGED, AND THE COUNT OF HIS STANDS ON THAT WET PAVEMENT IS CLOSED AT EIGHT. THE ROTA HAS NOT BEEN REBUILT AND CANNOT BE, THE 11:00–13:30 ROW IS STILL EMPTY AND STILL DOES NOT SAY *VACANT*, BRAM OSEI'S THURSDAY IS STILL REFUSED IN HIS OWN HANDWRITING WITH A SECOND LINE UNDER IT IN SOMEBODY ELSE'S HAND THAT NOBODY LOOKED AT IN ANY OF THE FIFTY CHAPTERS OF THIS VOLUME, AND THE ORDER DID NOT REBUILD A ROTA AND DID NOT LOOK AT THE SECOND LINE.**
- **AND A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED, AND IT IS THE TWENTY-THIRD OF SEPTEMBER 2015, AND A CITY LEARNED HOW TO COUNT EVERYBODY IT NEEDS IN AUGUST, AND NINE PEOPLE SAID YES IN A DOORWAY AND IT DID NOT HELP ONE OF THEM, AND A PRINTED SHEET WENT ROUND A BUILDING TWICE WITH AN EMPTY COLUMN ON THE SECOND COPY AND HELPED NOBODY, AND THE SHAPE OF THE CITY IS NOW ON FORTY WALLS AND HAS HELPED NOBODY, AND BEING ON FORTY WALLS IS NOT BEING COUNTED, AND IT IS NOT REFUTED, AND IT IS HEAVIER, AND NOTHING IN THIS BOROUGH IS GOING TO ANSWER IT.**
- **AND FOUR QUESTIONS ARE STILL OPEN AND NONE OF THEM WAS ANSWERED BY THE CLOSE, WHICH ASKED NO NEW ONE: from 0184, which of the dated things this borough has done once is the first one somebody will be able to prove, and whether by a person who was standing there or by one who reads about it afterwards; from 0174, which of the eight dated things is the first one somebody decides to keep, and whether it is kept because it was good or because it was expensive; from 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first one somebody asks a person to agree to; from 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city is going to have to stop, and what a person has to be holding to stop each of them, the second of which is nothing. AND THE ELEVEN STANDING REFUSALS ARE ELEVEN AND ARE NOT TWELVE, AND NOTHING THAT HAPPENED IN VOLUME 04 WAS A TWELFTH.**

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 204 — Tuesday the tenth of November 2015, after seven in the evening

**A MUTUAL-AID PERMISSION FROM ANOTHER CITY IS IN FORCE AND IT IS THE FIRST DOCUMENT IN NINE MONTHS WITH A PERSON'S NAME ON IT, AND A CONTINUITY ORDER MADE IN AUGUST IS IN FORCE, AND THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THOSE TWO THINGS IS THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN A PERMISSION AND AN OBLIGATION, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS WORKED OUT WHICH OF THE TWO THE MEN ON THE PERMISSION WERE HANDED.** Everything that happened in seven weeks in this borough was correct and none of it is written down except five words on the back of a bread docket in a drawer that has no lock on it.

- **THE PERMISSION.** Two pages, reference AM/15/221, between this city and a city called Alderwick on the other side of the water. A person named in the schedule may be asked to work in the other city for not exceeding fourteen days; a person asked takes the work or does not take it at the hour the work begins; a person named is paid the printed going rate in money in full before the work begins; a person named keeps a right of return on the day the work ends without asking anybody for anything; where a person is receiving care in one city the other city pays for it and the person does not move; a claim arising out of work done under it is made in the city where the work was done and not in the city the person came from; and nothing in it requires a person to be asked, to be found, to be named or to be told anything. **THE SCHEDULE CARRIES NINE NAMES. IT IS SIGNED AT BOTH ENDS AND THERE IS NO LINE UNDER EITHER SIGNATURE WITH NOTHING IN IT, AND SOMEBODY IN THIS CITY SIGNED IT AT A COUNTER IN A BUILDING BY THE WATER AND NOBODY COULD SAY WHICH OF THE PEOPLE ON THAT COUNTER.** It is not in a building in this city, it is in a plastic wallet, and it went back across the water on the six.
- **THE MAN WHO WENT.** A man of about thirty-seven with his own tipper and a flatbed, on nothing, went on 6 October for fourteen days, was told at ten past six in the morning at a yard entrance, had said yes on the Saturday, and the money was in an envelope in the cab before he started. **He is all right, he is paid, and he was told he would be back on the twentieth and came back on the twentieth and goes out on Thursdays. THIS BOROUGH HAS NEVER HAD A MECHANISM FOR A PERSON COMING BACK AND THERE IS NOT GOING TO BE ONE.** He has not been asked one question by anybody in either city and is not a thread.
- **THE MAN WHO WAS FOUND.** **EWEN ASHGROVE, ABOUT SIXTY-TWO**, walked out of a room in Kilburn Street in the second week of April and has been at Alderwick since 11 August on fourteen-day contracts renewed six times. **He came across the estuary on Monday 2 November on his own account, having been told his details had gone to a city he had left, to find out why. On 3 November he said the sentence himself: NOBODY ASKED ME TO BE FOUND. He said it twice. Nobody objected.** He said it had cost him nothing, and that he would not go back to Kilburn Street on a Tuesday, and that the second half is worse: **a doctor in a town he cannot name, paid for out of a council that has never heard of him, under a clause that is correct, which he cannot say no to and would like to.** About ninety pounds of his is in a tin in a drawer in Kilburn Street and the man who let the room was told on 24 October that he cannot act on any part of it and did not.
- **THE RAIN.** From the west, on the night of Sunday 11 October, heavy until about half past seven on the Monday and over by about eleven. The run at the bottom of Peverell Street went from six inches to two feet. **Nobody died, nobody went to hospital, nobody was sent for, nobody was fetched. The water had gone out of the city by the Wednesday and the river was down about two feet by the tenth of November.** At about ten to five somebody pulled three boards off the bottom of an old brick base of a fence and the water went through and **the wall stood** and two doorways stayed dry and a yard next door took about a foot and a man of about seventy-seven lost a shelf of stock. **Nobody can find out who and nobody is going to be asked, and the boards went under the run and were not seen again.**
- **THE THING THAT WAS WRITTEN.** Friday 30 October. A woman of about sixty-four who has worked behind a canteen hatch for six years and is on nothing opened the hatch on her own time for a man of about thirty-one who comes at ten to three, and wrote five words on the back of a bread delivery docket — *Fri. hatch open. till 6.* — and folded it once and put it in a drawer under the counter and shut the drawer. **That is the first time in nine months a correct thing in this borough has come with a piece of paper on it and the person who paid for the paper was the person who did the thing. Jonas Mercer saw her do it, she knew he saw her do it, and neither of them said a word.**
- **THE THING THAT WAS ASKED.** Saturday 7 November, a hall off a parade of shops, about nine people, daylight. **A woman of about forty-two who has the key to that hall asked him which square on the drawing is the empty one and what is supposed to go in it, because she had been asked four times that week and is the one who has to stand in that room. HE SAID HE DOES NOT KNOW, AND THAT HE READ THE SHEET ON 18 SEPTEMBER BEFORE IT WENT ON ANY WALL, AND THAT IT HAS BEEN EMPTY ON EVERY COPY SINCE, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO GUESS. THAT IS THE FIRST TIME IN SEVEN WEEKS HE HAS SAID ANYTHING ABOUT IT TO ANYBODY. THE SQUARE IS NOT FILLED. HE WAS GIVEN NOTHING.** A room can hold a sentence and a room cannot hold a person, and the sentence is in the room and cannot be got back out of it.
- **THE LAST THING.** Tuesday 10 November, about a quarter past seven in the evening, the low end of Peverell Street. **A man of about fifty-four with a handcart and a shovel cleared a gully outside a door, and a woman of about eighty-five came out with a stiff broom and a bucket and swept what he had put on the pavement down to the kerb, and they worked beside each other for about an hour, and neither of them asked the other for anything, and the whole of what was said between them was that a bar is not a brush and that that is a survey and not a criticism.** Neither of them is on anything and nobody is going to find out.
- **THE ORDER IS IN FORCE, ITS SIXTH CLAUSE IS STILL NOT BEING APPLIED, IT IS STILL NOT SETTLED, AND THE RENEWAL POINT OF WEDNESDAY 11 NOVEMBER 2015 IS ONE DAY AWAY AND WAS NOT REACHED, NOT NAMED AND NOT COUNTED DOWN IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS. NOBODY RENEWED IT, NOBODY EXPIRED IT, NOBODY RENAMED THE BLANK TERM, NOBODY PUT A NAME IN THE LINE ON PAGE TWO, AND NOT ONE WORD OF ITS SEVEN CLAUSES OR ITS PAGE TWO HAS BEEN ALTERED.** The register is at nineteen and the blank line under it is three hundred and forty-nine days and is still the only fixed point in that building, and the maintenance on entry nineteen is a bulb and the bulb is out, and **a drawing, a permission, a rain, a flood, a man going and a man being found are all not reasons and nobody is obliged.** The card in the slot is two hundred and sixty-three days and nobody has taken it out. The letter in the tray on the second floor is two hundred and sixty-five days and is still sealed under two other pieces of post and **no chapter of this batch went near that room.** A drawer under the ground-floor counter has no key on it and no list and the correct sentence a woman put in it on the morning of 20 May 2015 is still in it and nobody in this borough knows it is there, and no chapter of this batch went near that drawer. The counter is not a service and is not in a scene in any of the ten chapters and answered nothing and gave no count to anybody.
- **AND THE BOROUGH'S OWN POSITION, WHICH IS NOT REFUTED BY ANYTHING THAT HAPPENED IN SEVEN WEEKS: A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED.** A man is on a schedule, a contract, a rent and a doctor's card in two cities and has been asked one thing by either. A man is on a telephone number written on a folded piece of A4 and a rate printed in another city's schedule. Nine people said yes in a doorway in August. A printed sheet went round a building with nine lines reading *no requirement stated*. The shape of the city is on more walls than it was in September and on the inside of a hall. **Being on walls is not being counted, and being counted by another city's document is not being asked, and the eleven standing refusals are eleven and a permission is not a twelfth.**
- **AND THE OPEN QUESTION, WHICH IS THE BATCH'S OWN, WHICH IS NOT ABOUT A PERSON, AND WHICH IS NOT ANSWERED: A PERMISSION IS A THING A PERSON MAY DO. AN OBLIGATION IS A THING A PERSON HAS TO DO. THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN LIVING ON THE SECOND ONE FOR NINE MONTHS AND WAS HANDED THE FIRST ONE IN PRINT EIGHTEEN DAYS EARLIER, WHICH IS WEDNESDAY 30 SEPTEMBER TO SUNDAY 18 OCTOBER AND WAS MEASURED RATHER THAN CARRIED FORWARD, AND NOBODY HAS WORKED OUT WHAT EITHER OF THE MEN ON IT WAS HANDED. IT IS THE TENTH OF NOVEMBER AND IT IS OPEN.** The four questions Volume 04 accumulated are all still open and none of them was answered.

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 214 — Wednesday the thirtieth of December 2015, after about nine in the evening, in a city that is not this one

**THE ORDER IS NOT IN FORCE AND EVERYONE WHO WAS EVER GOING TO BE TOLD WHY WAS TOLD ON THE ELEVENTH OF NOVEMBER, AND NOTHING ELSE HAPPENED THAT DAY, AND THE FIELD IS A COLUMN IN A BOOK ON A TABLE. FOUR PEOPLE WENT ACROSS AN ESTUARY IN NOVEMBER AND THE FIRST OF THE THREE REGIONAL SYSTEMS HAS BEEN STOOD IN, AND IT WORKS, AND EVERY PART OF IT BELONGS TO SOMEBODY PRIVATELY, AND NOBODY IN IT IS HIDING ANYTHING.**

- **THE ORDER.** Made in this city on Thursday 13 August 2015, in force for fifty-four days after the sixth clause was stopped on 18 September, and **NOT IN FORCE SINCE ABOUT NINE IN THE MORNING OF WEDNESDAY 11 NOVEMBER 2015. ALL SEVEN CLAUSES STOPPED AT ONCE. IT WAS NOT RENEWED, NOT EXPIRED, NOT CANCELLED, NOT SUSPENDED, NOT AMENDED, NOT EXTENDED, NOT REPLACED, NOT PUBLISHED IN FULL AND NOT QUOTED AT LENGTH, AND NOT ONE WORD OF ITS SEVEN CLAUSES OR ITS PAGE TWO WAS ALTERED. NOBODY PUT A NAME IN THE LINE UNDER THE DATE AND THE BLANK TERM WAS NOT RENAMED.** The mechanism: a clause that says an order is renewed, or is not renewed, needs a person, and there is no body in this city that can require a person, and there are two people here with a department, one of whom made it and one of whom did not, and **neither is going to be asked to sign anything.**
- **THE FIELD.** Not emptied, not wiped, no line through anything and no note in the margin. A working book about two inches deep on a table by the wall in a building by the water, a column ruled in pencil: a name, the hour called, the hour home, and the thing the person was called for. **The last entry is the fourteenth of September 2015, which is the last time anybody in this city was called on. The number of names in it is not given to anybody and has not been counted.**
- **WHAT IT COST, AND WHO WAS TOLD.** For fifty-four days the only reason Jonas Mercer had anything to do about clause six was that the order was in force to be stopped. **A man of about twenty-three with a back that has been bad since the spring, who came out on 18 September because a hand went on him, is not a man who has been let go. His back is in nobody's field because there is no field. Fifty-four days after the hand went on, nobody in this city was told that this is what happened, and nobody could be told.** Three people know: Jonas Mercer, Nina Calder the records supervisor, who wrote nothing down, and Tomas Renn, who was told on a boat and refused to say the rest of it out loud because then it would be in Jonas's mouth for the rest of his life.
- **WHAT A LAPSE LEAVES.** Nothing visible, and that is the finding. Fifty minutes on foot on 17 November and no gap anywhere. **A man of about seventy-three, called nine times since 22 August, paid the going rate, has declined nothing, was not called again, and looks different in a way nobody could name: he had his coat over his arm and nothing in his hands.** A man of about twenty-eight came back to a counter he had been at in June and asked whether there is anywhere a person can put the low end of Peverell Street, and was told twice in the same words that there is no form for that, and said *I know*, and that was the whole of it. **The counter answered nothing and gave no count and was not asked about the order.**
- **THE MAJOR MOVEMENT, AND THE FIRST OF THE THREE REGIONAL SYSTEMS.** A one-page letter from a standing group of three authorities that runs the water for three towns in a city called Ravensmere arrived on 20 November and invited a man to sit in a room, and its fourth paragraph recorded him as that group's correspondent, which is not true and which he refused in writing. **He said out loud, to three people, before anybody asked him anything, that he was going as a person who is there and not as anybody's delegate, and the arrangement has no word for that.** On Sunday 29 November four of them crossed the estuary on the ten-past-seven boat, and a woman said in a doorway at half past six that she is not going to be fixed and is not going to be the reason anybody is talked out of anything, and that she is on that boat because there is a boat and a room on the Friday. **Nothing about that is repaired.**
- **THE FIRST SYSTEM AND THE TWO LINES.** It works, it is cheap, it is correct, it has not failed. The works was built before anybody in that room was born. A company owns the works in the first town, a trust owns the mains in the second, and in the third one pumping station is municipal and the other two are not, and the two that are not have both changed hands inside ten years with nothing broken and nothing promised and the obligations left exactly where they were. **Nobody in it is a villain, nobody is hiding anything, and every sentence the clerk says is right, including the word *deliberate*, out loud.** At the bottom of the sheet, in the same type, the same size, with the same space above each: *Nothing is required of any person in order to use any part of this system* and *A person has no standing in respect of anything recorded under this.* **The corridor wall sheet carries only the first line. The office sheet carries both. The short one has had a line taken off it and has not been left blank.**
- **THE SENTENCE, AND ITS STANDING.** On 15 December a woman of about fifty-one with nineteen years in that building said the true thing about the bargain into a corridor, to one person, could not finish it, said it anyway, and then said she should not have said it: the part everybody is responsible for will always be the part somebody owns, and the part nobody owns is the part that gets sold. **She could not finish it because in that room the sentence is an accusation and it is not an accusation. He said nothing at all, the notebook stayed in the inside pocket of his coat, and on the Friday he told a lawyer that nothing was wrong with it and the water was good. The standing of that sentence in this account is exactly the standing of a sentence said once on a pavement in September.**
- **THE CLAIM THAT CAME BACK.** One sheet, dated 11 December 2015, carrying the reference of the permission, made in Alderwick under the sixth clause and correctly made, naming a man of about twenty who worked fourteen days at a pumping station on the north bank in October and was injured on the last day of the fortnight, for a sum that is about what a man of that age costs to keep for about a month. **It has come to the city that granted the permission, which is the city the person came from, and it cannot be dealt with here, and it is in a tray under a window in a records room.** Nobody in this borough can tell whether it is a price, a receipt or a precedent, and all three readings are true. Jonas Mercer is the first person to see what it is and did not say so. **By the time somebody else saw it there were two people in two cities and no document in either that says either of them knew.**
- **AN ORDINARY SUNDAY.** Two small correct things in a city that is not this one, four hundred yards apart on the same street, done by two people who are on nothing, and nobody found out, and the only person who knows both walked down the street twice. A label on a shelf in a chemist's and a timetable of the wrong year in a bag. **This is the third time since the pump at the bottom of Peverell Street that a correct thing has been done in a street by somebody on nothing and nothing has come of it.**
- **AND THE BOROUGH'S OWN POSITION, WHICH IS NOT REFUTED BY ANYTHING THAT HAPPENED IN SEVEN WEEKS OR IN EIGHT MONTHS: A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED.** A man is on a schedule in two cities and a doctor's card in one. A man is on a telephone number on a folded piece of paper. A man of about thirty-one has a canteen hatch and a corridor in another city and has told nobody. A man of about thirty-nine is paid by a city for work in his own city and is called a relief contract on one bank and an attachment on the other. A man of about twenty has an injury described in two lines by somebody who did not have to look at him. Six people came home from a fortnight with dates on a sheet. **Being on walls is not being counted, being counted by another city's document is not being asked, and the eleven standing refusals are eleven and a lapse is not a twelfth and a permission is not a twelfth and a regional system is not a twelfth and a boat is not a twelfth and a claim is not a twelfth.**
- **AND THE OPEN QUESTIONS, ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE ANSWERED.** From 0184, which dated thing will be the first somebody can prove. From 0174, which of the eight will be the first somebody decides to keep. From 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first a person is asked to agree to, and a letter that records a man as somebody's correspondent is a fourth candidate and he refused it. From 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city has to stop, and the second of them is still nothing. And the batch's own, restated on 30 December as a fact about two pieces of paper: **which of the two the people were handed.** And a sixth, new, and about a document and not a person: **a claim has arrived, it is correct, it is in the right city, it is in a tray in a borough with no standing to answer it, and two people in two cities know what it is and neither has said.** The register is at nineteen and the blank line under it is three hundred and ninety-nine days and is still the only thing in that building that has not moved. The card in the slot is three hundred and thirteen days. The letter in the tray on the second floor is three hundred and fifteen days and no chapter of this batch went near that room.

Volume 05, February to April 2016 — **BATCH 0004'S CALENDAR, COUNTED FROM THE SAME ANCHOR AND BINDING ON BATCH 0005: Sunday 28 February · Sunday 6 March · Saturday 12 March · Friday 18 March · Tuesday 22 March · Sunday 27 March · Friday 1 April · Wednesday 6 April · Monday 11 April · Saturday 16 April 2016. THE TEN CARDS RAN IN THAT ORDER, THE GAPS ARE 7, 7, 6, 6, 4, 5, 5, 5, 5 AND 5 DAYS, ALL INSIDE THE THREE-TO-SEVEN WINDOW, AND EVERY WEEKDAY WAS CHECKED AGAINST THE REAL 2016 CALENDAR. NO CHAPTER IS DATED BEFORE SUNDAY 28 FEBRUARY 2016 AND NO CHAPTER IS DATED TO A DAY BEFORE THE ONE BEFORE IT.**

**THE THREE DAY-COUNTS AT EACH OF THOSE TEN DATES, ADDED UP FROM THE ANCHORS OF WEDNESDAY 26 NOVEMBER 2014, FRIDAY 20 FEBRUARY 2015 AND WEDNESDAY 18 FEBRUARY 2015, AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM ANY SUMMARY: THE BLANK LINE UNDER THE NINETEENTH 459 · 466 · 472 · 478 · 482 · 487 · 492 · 497 · 502 · **507**; THE CARD IN THE SLOT 373 · 380 · 386 · 392 · 396 · 401 · 406 · 411 · 416 · **421**; THE LETTER IN THE TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR 375 · 382 · 388 · 394 · 398 · 403 · 408 · 413 · 418 · **423**. ONLY THE LAST OF THE FIRST LIST APPEARS IN THE PROSE OF CHAPTERS 225–234, AND IT IS IN 0234, AND IT IS THE FIGURE ON A PIECE OF PAPER IN A MAN'S INSIDE POCKET.**

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 224 — Sunday the twenty-first of February 2016, after eleven in the morning, in this city
