**THE ORDER IS NOT IN FORCE AND NOTHING HAS BEEN DONE ABOUT IT FOR NINETY-THREE DAYS, AND A TOWN EIGHT MILES UP THE ESTUARY OWNS EVERYTHING IT HAS AND STAFFS IT WITH ONE MAN OF EIGHTY-SIX WHO WAS ASKED ONCE, IN A ROOM, IN A SPRING, AND SAID YES, AND HE IS STILL RAKING AN INTAKE BY HAND AT HALF PAST FIVE IN THE DARK.**

- **THE ORDER.** Made in this city on Thursday 13 August 2015, **NOT IN FORCE SINCE ABOUT NINE IN THE MORNING OF WEDNESDAY 11 NOVEMBER 2015, ALL SEVEN CLAUSES AT ONCE, NOT RENEWED, NOT EXPIRED, NOT CANCELLED, NOT SUSPENDED, NOT AMENDED, NOT EXTENDED, NOT REPLACED, NOT PUBLISHED IN FULL AND NOT QUOTED AT LENGTH, AND NOT ONE WORD OF ITS SEVEN CLAUSES OR ITS PAGE TWO HAS BEEN ALTERED. NOBODY PUT A NAME IN THE LINE UNDER THE DATE AND THE BLANK TERM WAS NOT RENAMED.** The field is a column in a working book about two inches deep on a table by the wall and its last entry is the fourteenth of September 2015 and its number of names is not given to anybody and has not been counted. Three people know why and all three were in this city when they were told and none of them is going to be asked again.
- **THE SECOND REGIONAL SYSTEM, AND IT IS THE MIRROR OF THE FIRST.** In Ravensmere the ownership is private and the obligation is public and there is nobody to hold it. In a town called Marrowgate, eight miles up the estuary, the works, the clinic, the shelter, the gate and the yard all belong to the town, a house there has a water bill of about ninety pence a quarter, nobody who lives there has ever been asked for a card, and the whole bill is a seventh of what the same water costs on this side of the water. **IT IS CHEAPER THAN THE FIRST SYSTEM AND BETTER, IT HAS NOT FAILED, NOBODY IN IT IS A VILLAIN AND NOBODY IS HIDING ANYTHING, AND THE TOWN'S WHOLE ARGUMENT IS THAT A DUTY IS AN ACT AND NOT A PLACE, AND A PLACE CANNOT BE GIVEN TO A PERSON, AND THEREFORE THE PERSON CANNOT BE REPLACED, AND THEREFORE NOBODY HAS EVER ASKED HIM.** Every step of that is true and the last one is never drawn out loud.
- **THE ARRANGEMENT IS FOUR LINES ON A WALL AND CANNOT BE PRODUCED FROM THIS BOROUGH.** Jonas Mercer asked for a copy on 22 January and refused one himself, on the grounds that it would be a sheet somebody brought and not the sheet on the wall. **A MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-SIX IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THAT TOWN WHO COULD READ A PUMP HOUSE, STRIP A STOPCOCK, RELIGHT THE SHELTER BOILER, WORK THE INTAKE RACK AND CHANGE THE CHAIN ON THE YARD GATE, AND HE IS NOT THE ONLY PERSON WHO DOES ANY OF IT, AND HE FOUND THAT OUT ON THE WEDNESDAY AFTERNOON OF 12 JANUARY AND TOLD NOBODY.** He was not rescued, he was not asked to stop, nothing was arranged for him, and nobody is going to ask him whether he would like to stop.
- **WHAT THE SECOND SYSTEM COSTS, AND WHO PAYS IT.** There is no tank big enough, so while the intake is raked the whole town is dry, and a woman of about eighty who takes in washing has stopped washing on those two days without telling anybody. In the February of the year before, nine days of the man being off cost this town a cloudy Thursday, a clinic shut for most of a day, a gate shut because nobody had a key, a shelter boiler out at eleven on a Sunday night that a man of about ninety-one got going at about half past one after two goes, a fortnight of no washing, and a bill of three days of a man from outside, a fortnight of the man's wages and a tank. **THE FIGURE FOR THE WORKS HAS NOT MOVED IN ELEVEN JANUARIES BECAUSE THERE IS NO NINE DAYS LINE AND BECAUSE IT HAS NEVER NOT HAPPENED. THE CORRECT SENTENCE ABOUT THAT SOUNDS LIKE AN OBJECTION, FOUR PEOPLE IN THAT ROOM THOUGHT IT AT THE SAME MOMENT, NOBODY PUT IT IN THE ROOM, AND JONAS MERCER WAS IN THE CORRIDOR OUTSIDE IT ALL DAY AND IS THE ONLY PERSON IN TWO COUNTRIES WHO COULD HAVE.**
- **THE THREE SENTENCES THAT HAVE NOW BEEN SAID ONCE, AND NOT TWICE.** A sentence on a pavement in September 2015. A sentence in a corridor in December 2015, by a woman with a folder on a window ledge, which was not finished. And a sentence at twenty past six on Wednesday 17 February 2016 in a back room at the works, by a man of about eighty-six, about himself, which was finished, and which was followed immediately by him saying he should not have said it. **ALL THREE WERE SAID ONCE, NONE WAS WRITTEN DOWN, NONE WAS REPEATED, NONE WAS MINUTED, AND THE THIRD TIME IS NOT A PATTERN AND NOBODY SAYS IT IS A PATTERN.**
- **THE DELEGATION, AND THE BOUNDARY.** On 28 January Jonas Mercer said no to a woman with a plastic wallet, handed the work to a shop steward who had agreed to be nothing, and said out loud that he had run out of a thing he was good at; the work was done differently and not worse, he said *you are right* and *there is nothing I can put next to it*, and nobody gave anybody a title. On 2 February, in a corner by a rack of leaflets, a woman set a personal boundary about something that is not the work, and he accepted it in one sentence and did not ask why. **IT WAS NOT A REPAIR AND IT WAS NOT A RUPTURE AND IT WAS ALSO NOT NOTHING, AND NOTHING ABOUT THE SEPTEMBER ARGUMENT IS SETTLED, AND HER TWENTY MINUTES A WEEK AND THE COMPLIANCE PARAGRAPH BEHIND IT ARE UNTOUCHED.**
- **THE CLAIM, TWENTY-SIX DAYS IN THE DRAWER.** One sheet dated 11 December 2015, made in Alderwick under the sixth clause and correctly made, about a man of about twenty who worked fourteen days at a pumping station in October and was hurt on the last of them. It came in on 26 December, went in a tray, went up with the water office's post, sat on a shelf until Tuesday 26 January, and is in a drawer in a locked room at the end of a first-floor corridor. **IT IS A PRICE AND IT IS A RECEIPT AND IT IS A PRECEDENT AND ALL THREE ARE STILL TRUE, AND THE ONLY THING THAT HAS CHANGED IS THAT SOMEBODY IN ANOTHER CITY HAS READ IT, WHICH IS IN A LETTER ISELIN KROGH WROTE ON FRIDAY 5 FEBRUARY 2016, WHICH CAME ON SATURDAY THE SIXTH, AND THAT HE HAS READ FOUR TIMES AND NOT ANSWERED.** A tray under a window is not a register and does not become one by being emptied.
- **THE FOURTH CORRECT THING.** A bench moved a foot and a half from a parish notice board by a man with a barrow on the saying of a man of about ninety, and a hardware shop's door sign that said half past eight changed to a quarter to eight by a woman of about eighty-three, four hundred yards apart, both done by people on nothing, nobody finding out. **IT IS THE FOURTH SINCE THE PUMP AT THE BOTTOM OF PEVERELL STREET, AND ALL THREE OF THE EARLIER ONES ARE NAMED IN 0217, AND THE ONLY PERSON WHO KNOWS ALL FOUR IS A MAN WHO STANDS IN CORRIDORS.**
- **AND THE BOROUGH'S OWN POSITION, WHICH IS NOT REFUTED BY ANYTHING THAT HAPPENED IN A YEAR AND A NINE MONTHS: A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED.** A man is on a schedule in two cities and a doctor's card in one. A man is on a telephone number on a folded piece of paper. A man of about eighty-six is on a rate roll in a town he was born in and nobody in this borough can produce the sheet that says so. A man of about twenty has an injury described in two lines by somebody who did not have to look at him. **BEING ON WALLS IS NOT BEING COUNTED, BEING COUNTED BY ANOTHER CITY'S DOCUMENT IS NOT BEING ASKED, AND THE ELEVEN STANDING REFUSALS ARE ELEVEN AND A LAPSE IS NOT A TWELFTH AND A PERMISSION IS NOT A TWELFTH AND A CLAIM IS NOT A TWELFTH AND A REGIONAL SYSTEM IS NOT A TWELFTH AND A BOAT IS NOT A TWELFTH AND A TOWN THAT OWNS EVERYTHING IS NOT A TWELFTH AND A MAN WHO HOLDS FIVE THINGS IS NOT A TWELFTH.**
- **AND THE OPEN QUESTIONS, ALL SIX STILL OPEN AND NONE ANSWERED.** From 0184, which dated thing will be the first somebody can prove. From 0174, which of the eight will be the first somebody decides to keep. From 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first a person is asked to agree to, and two letters that are both binding and neither of which cancels the other is a fourth candidate. From 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city has to stop, and the second of them is still nothing. And the batch's own, restated on 21 February as a fact about two documents in two hands in two cities: **which of the two the people were handed.** And the sixth, about a document and not a person: **a claim in a drawer for twenty-six days, and two days of it in a tray over Christmas and the rest of it on a shelf in a building by the water, and two people in two cities who know what it is and neither has said.** The register is at nineteen and the blank line under it is four hundred and fifty-two days and is still the only thing in that building that has not moved while an instrument went out of force in it, and that is worse than a relief and is not a relief. The card in the slot is three hundred and sixty-six days. The letter in the tray on the second floor is three hundred and sixty-eight days and no chapter of this batch went near that room. **There is a third of the three regional systems somewhere and nobody in this borough has seen it and he is not going this month.**

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 234 — Saturday the sixteenth of April 2016, from about eleven in the morning, in this city

**THE ORDER IS NOT IN FORCE AND NOTHING HAS BEEN DONE ABOUT IT FOR ONE HUNDRED AND FIFTY-SEVEN DAYS, ALL THREE REGIONAL SYSTEMS HAVE NOW BEEN STOOD IN, THE MIDPOINT REVERSAL IS VISIBLE AND IS NOT A VILLAIN, AND A VAN WENT OUT OF A SHOP ON THIS PARADE FIVE WEEKS AND FOUR DAYS AGO WITH THREE PEOPLE IN IT WHO HAD NOT BEEN ASKED.**

- **THE ORDER.** Made in this city on Thursday 13 August 2015, **NOT IN FORCE SINCE ABOUT NINE IN THE MORNING OF WEDNESDAY 11 NOVEMBER 2015, ALL SEVEN CLAUSES AT ONCE, NOT RENEWED, NOT EXPIRED, NOT CANCELLED, NOT SUSPENDED, NOT AMENDED, NOT EXTENDED, NOT REPLACED, NOT PUBLISHED IN FULL AND NOT QUOTED AT LENGTH, AND NOT ONE WORD OF ITS SEVEN CLAUSES OR ITS PAGE TWO HAS BEEN ALTERED. NOBODY PUT A NAME IN THE LINE UNDER THE DATE AND THE BLANK TERM WAS NOT RENAMED.** It is not mentioned by name in any chapter of chapters 225–234; what is said about it in two of them is *an instrument went out of force*. The field is a column in a working book on a table in a building by the water, about two inches deep, and its last entry is the fourteenth of September 2015, and the number of things in that column has not been given to anybody and has not been counted.
- **THE THIRD REGIONAL SYSTEM, AND IT IS THE THIRD OF THREE AND THE BEST OF THE THREE.** Penhale is not a town. It is a bend in a road with a bus stop, about forty houses and four vans, and a shared yard on the flat part, and there is no church in it and no square and no pub. **THE ARRANGEMENT OWNS NOTHING AT ALL: THE SHED IS HIRED BY THE WEEK, THE RACKING IS HIRED BY THE WEEK, THE PIPE IS SOLD TO THE CREW AND CARRIED OUT BY THE CREW, THE TWO TRUCKS BELONG TO A HAULIER IN THE NEXT COUNTY, THE OFFICE IS A HUT ON A BASE, THE BENCH IS A BENCH, THE FENCE BELONGS TO A FARM NEXT DOOR, AND THE POOL IS MADE OF WHAT NINE YARDS PUT IN AND EVERY ONE OF THE NINE BELONGS TO SOMEBODY.**
- **THE RULE, AND IT IS ONE SENTENCE, AND IT IS A RULE AND NOT A THING.** Framed on the wall by the office door at an angle nobody has straightened: any part of any system in this region may be used by any person. **A second sheet in a rack carries a second line under the first in the same type: a person has no standing in respect of anything recorded under this. Both are off one original, 1987, neither has been reprinted, and the reason the second line exists is that a name against a job would make the yard a business, and a business could be invoiced, and a business is the one thing the arrangement is built not to be.** Nobody's required to be one of the nine yards, and about four of the nine have never sent or taken a crew and pay in the same.
- **HOW IT IS PAID FOR, AND WHAT THE PAPER IS.** Parts, fuel, hire and a yard that is somebody's. The book is a carbon book: top copy, carbon, and a shelf, and the top copies go in a box under a bed. The wall sheet is A1 and the heads are JOB, PLACE, DAYS, PARTS, FUEL, HIRE and CLAIM, and above the top-left box, where a person would put a name, it says LEVEL OF SERVICE and the rest of the box has been empty since it was printed. **The scheme puts a crew on a job anywhere in the region for nothing, and the job carries a piece of paper at the end of it, and the paper is not an invoice: it is a claim form, the same form for every job, and the sum on it is not what the job cost. Nobody in that yard can say no to anybody and nobody there has ever asked a crew what a job ought to cost.**
- **THE INHERITED CLAIM, AND THE YEAR, AND WHY NOBODY HAS ANYONE TO ASK.** The sum is the sum the region agreed in 1987, the work was done for that sum then, and every job since has been done for that sum. **The rate has not been revised because nobody who is owed the difference is still in any of the bodies that agreed it: to move a number you need a person who loses, and there is no person who loses, and everybody who agreed 1987 is dead or has left or has moved on to the next thing.** The people in that yard have known the year was old since about 1994 and have never been asked what happens when the year is old, because that question is a question to a body and there is no body.
- **THE DRAIN, WHICH IS NOT THE SUM AND NOT THE PAPER.** Eleven claims: £140, £152, £163, £171, £168, £174, £112, £180, £166, £190 and £196, which is **£1,812**, added up twice on the back of an order pad and the same both times, over the span since the spring, the first job being the week the water went down. **The sum is small and the paper is correct. The drain is that every job a crew from this borough does in that region puts a claim against this borough that nobody in this borough can answer, measure, refuse or plan for, and the arrangement is entirely voluntary and is still joined by people who want to join it. A borough that sends crews into an arrangement that works that way has agreed, without a meeting and without a signature, that it has no counterparty.**
- **THE FOUR COMPANIES, AND THE THREE WEEKS.** Corrance Supply, Sarn Aggregates, Lowdale Hire and Marchmont. All four sell into the shared yard, all four are correct, and not one of them has ever been asked a question. The Corrance counter has had them nine years as her biggest single account and has never been late and does not know the rate. The Lowdale cabin says half his turnover in this borough is trade that goes out of it and comes back on a Sunday because the same crews take it out again, and calls it *a Tuesday*. The Marchmont forecourt is about a tenth above the yard and has been repainted twice. **A borough that decided its crews did no more work over the water would find, in about three weeks, that its own insurance, its own parts, its own hire and sometimes its own fuel were more expensive, and nobody would have had to do anything hostile, and the people who would have enjoyed it are people this borough has never met.**
- **THE SCHEDULE OF ELEVEN NAMES, IN THE SAME ROOM AS THE CLAIM.** It came up with the Friday tray on 1 April and had not been opened. It is a single sheet with a rule across it and a column headed CLAIM and eleven ruled lines, and in each line a date, a place, a name and a sum, and the yard had got all eleven sums right. **It is the first thing that has ever come back along a document to this borough that names a person by name, by date and by place, and it is correct, and it is a carbon and not a bill, and nothing on it can be invoiced for and nobody at the other end is waiting for an answer. All eleven can be named and none of the eleven was asked. The man of about thirty-one knows two of them and has said so to nobody and was not rung.**
- **THE CLAIM, NINETY-SIX DAYS IN THE DRAWER, AND IT IS STILL A CLAIM.** One sheet dated 11 December 2015, made in Alderwick under the sixth clause and correctly made, about a man of about twenty who worked fourteen days at a pumping station in October and was hurt on the last of them. It came in on 26 December, went in a tray under a window, went up with the water office's post on the Monday the twenty-eighth, sat on a shelf by the filing cabinets for a month, and is in a drawer in a locked room at the end of a first-floor corridor. **Twenty-eight of the ninety-six days are in the drawer, thirty are on the shelf, two were over Christmas in the tray, and the rest is in the post. A tray under a window is a tray under a window and is not a register. THE WOMAN WHO HAS THAT ROOM HAS SAID OUT LOAD THAT SHE DOES NOT KNOW WHAT IT IS, AND IS RIGHT, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS GOING TO BE TOLD.**
- **THE CREW, AND THE SENTENCE.** On 12 March a woman of about twenty-three called Abby Marlow and two men in their thirties went out of a shop on this parade at ten past seven on a standpipe, a gully and a soakaway at eight houses with no council, and the pay was right and the parts were good and nobody asked any of them. **She said the sentence on a pavement at a quarter to eight and nobody refuted it: a person who is not counted is not a person who is helped, and that is not a punishment, that is nobody knowing. She also said she likes it here, and that she would not have had the money or the van or the learning, and that if anybody told her she had been done a favour she would not have it.**
- **THE BOROUGH'S OWN SENTENCE, SAID TWICE IN ONE EVENING AND HEARD AS AN OBJECTION.** In a hall off the parade on 6 April, a man of about forty-four said both halves — the sentence, and the fact that this borough has paid nothing since the spring and is about to be given a bill for it by another city and that they will not be wrong to work it out — and the room heard it as making an objection, and a woman of about thirty-six asked what he was asking for, and he opened his mouth and shut it. **And then she said the old one, flatly, with the pad shut on her knee, having been saying it in that room for about two years: a drawing on about forty walls, a pump in a doorway, a shovel and a broom, a label on a shelf, a bench moved, a sign put right with a marker, a hose nobody came for, and a man of about eighty-six raking an intake by hand. The town that owns all of those people owns nobody, and the region that will send the bill owns nobody either, and that is the same sentence twice and it is still true.**
- **THE SIX QUESTIONS, AND THE DOCUMENT THAT CANNOT BE PRODUCED.** In a Methodist hall on Carrow Lane on 11 April, all six were restated in order and none was answered, and a man in his sixties put a version on the table that would have settled questions four, five and half of two in a fortnight, **and said out loud, to nine people, that it rests on a piece of paper nobody in this borough can produce, and sat down, and named the temptation to add a seventh question and left it alone.** The card on that wall still carries both halves of the same sentence and neither was argued out of the other.
- **THE THREE PLACES, AND WHAT IS STILL UNSPENT.** Three pieces of paper, each in somebody else's keeping: the one-line sheet on a wall in a corridor in a city on the other side of the water, which is a thing a person may do; the four-line sheet on a wall in a room in a town up the estuary, which is a thing a person has to do and which nobody here can produce and which Jonas Mercer refused a copy of; and the folded sheet in the drawer, which is a thing somebody else sent and which may be answered or not answered. **The compact, the mutual right of return, the wider civic experiment, the other first administrators, the climax and the resolution are all still ahead and none of them has been spent and none of them has been anticipated in a document, a letter or a character's intention.**
- **AND EVERYTHING ELSE IS WHERE IT WAS.** The register is at nineteen, the nineteenth is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb and the bulb is out, and the line under it is **five hundred and seven days** and is still blank, and it is the single thing in that building that has not moved while an instrument went out of force in it, and that is worse than a relief and is not a relief. A card has been in a slot four hundred and twenty-one days and has not been taken out, put up or read. A letter has been in a tray on the second floor under two other pieces of post for four hundred and twenty-three days, sealed, and nobody has opened it in front of a second person, and no chapter of chapters 195–234 has gone near that room. The page of 29 May 2015 is not in force and its one reader has not been asked again. The nine printed sheets of 19 June still read *no requirement stated* and have no name box. The column headed *date seen again* is still empty. The counter in the vestibule answered nothing and gave no count and was not entered. The drawer under it is shut. The rota is not rebuilt and the 11:00–13:30 row is still empty. The second line under Bram Osei's Thursday has not been looked at. The board is at seven of nine. The ninth seat is fifty-four and unpaid and untold. The buff file of 14 October 2011 is not opened and four people have seen the bay number and the date on that cover. Paragraph 4.11 is not answered and the compliance week is not verified. Roy Albery does not walk again. **Nobody died, nobody was rescued, nothing was minuted, nobody renewed, expired, renamed or put a name in anything, and the eleven standing refusals are eleven.**

---

# REVIEW REPAIR — BATCH 0004 — WHAT WAS MEASURED, WHAT WAS CHANGED, AND WHAT IS STILL A HUMAN'S

**A REVIEW PASS RAN ON THE OUTPUT OF `workspace/volume-05/batch-0004` AND ITS STATE CHANGES, FOUNDED FOURTEEN ITEMS, AND THIS BLOCK IS THE RECORD OF WHAT WAS DONE ABOUT THEM. NO CHAPTER WAS RESTARTED, NO GOOD PROSE WAS REPLACED, AND THE PLANNED PLOT WAS NOT TOUCHED. THE REVERSAL IN 0229 AND 0230, THE TEN DATES, THE CAPS, THE MEASURING PASS, THE LATTICE NOTICES AND THE WHOLE SHAPE OF THE BATCH ARE AS THEY WERE WRITTEN.**

**REPAIRED IN THE CHAPTERS, THREE EDITS AND NO OTHERS:**

1. **Chapter 0231, a closed figure reached the page.** The sheet from Penhale was described as having *eleven rows*. **`ELEVEN ROWS` IS A CLOSED FIGURE AND IT BELONGS TO THE HARDBOARD CLIPBOARD ON THE FOURTH CHAIR**, with the first four rows written on in October by a man who then stopped. **IT IS NOW *RULED DOWN THE SIDE AND THERE ARE ELEVEN LINES OF IT*,** and the same correction has been carried into `state/continuity.md`, `state/open-threads.md`, `state/current.md` and `state/chapter-summaries.md`, so the next batch does not inherit the phrase.
2. **Chapter 0231, a container slipped.** Hanna Wray's eleven claims were said to be kept *in an envelope*. **THEY ARE IN A CARRIER BAG**, which is what 0228, 0229 and 0231 itself say twice, and the envelope is the one Jonas Mercer's single job sheet arrived in and Nell Ardent put in her own bag. NOW A CARRIER BAG.
3. **Chapter 0229, a place drifted inside one chapter.** The dateline and the body put the finding in a first-floor corridor, and the closing block called the same spot a landing. **IT IS NOW A FIRST-FLOOR CORRIDOR WITH A FIRE DOOR AT THE END OF IT**, which is what the chapter describes twenty feet away from the fire door.

**REPAIRED IN THE STATE FILES:**

4. **A state file narrated an act that is not on the page.** `character-state.md` said that on 22 March Nell Ardent **wrote down four names out of eleven**. **SHE DID NOT. NOBODY WRITES ANYTHING DOWN ON THAT COUNTER ON THE TWENTY-SECOND OF MARCH.** What is on the page is that she laid the eleven claims out in two rows, read them, took them into her bag, and said the aside about a person who copies things out in her own hand; the four names in her own hand are hers on 1 April, in the corridor, and she did not ask for the other seven. The line is cut and the correction is recorded in her own entry.
5. **The figure census was counted wrong and then closed wrongly.** Three aged figures were introduced, not four, and a header said **FOUR** and listed three. Worse, four separate files said **the census ran out**, and one of them, and then the next prompt, invented **six free men who were never free** to replace the three free women that are. **THE CENSUS DID NOT RUN OUT. THE FREE FIGURES AFTER BATCH 0004 ARE WOMEN 18, 87 AND 90 AND THERE IS NO FREE FIGURE IN MEN.** Corrected in `character-state.md`, `open-threads.md`, `batch-summaries.md`, `continuity.md` and in the Batch 0005 prompt, and the invented men are struck everywhere they appear. **Hanna Wray has no age and was never one of the free figures; she is counted rather than left uncounted.**
6. **Six bold spans in the new character-state block opened twice and closed once,** so four paragraphs rendered whole in bold and swallowed their trailing plain text. Repaired, and the two header paragraphs in that block with them.

**REPAIRED IN THE PROMPTS:**

7. **The Batch 0004 prompt pointed a writer at a block that does not exist.** It named the *VOLUME 05, BATCH 0003 — CHAPTERS 225–224* block as the hand-over; **Batch 0003 is 215–224.** Corrected.
8. **A motif was governed by two rules that contradicted each other.** *There is no form in this borough* was declared **at zero in all ten chapters** in the hard limits and permitted **twice per chapter** in the motif list. **The hard limit now says the motif cap governs and that the zero is a fact about the batch as written and not a second cap.**
9. **One finding was checked and is a false positive, and the card is left alone.** Card 0228 names **the man of about twenty-one**, and the same prompt's reading list names **the man of about twenty**. **THOSE ARE TWO DIFFERENT MEN AND BOTH ARE IN THE ACCOUNT:** the man of about twenty worked a fortnight at a pumping station, came home, and has a claim made about him; the man of about twenty-one is the young man with a clipboard and a plastic wallet who went round about forty doors on a street near the market on 28 July 2015. Merging them would have created the error it was meant to prevent. **NOTHING WAS CHANGED AND THIS IS RECORDED SO IT IS NOT RAISED AGAIN.**

**THE STRUCTURAL REPAIR, AND IT IS THE LARGEST OF THE FOURTEEN:**

10. **The state files are append-only and no longer loadable.** `continuity.md` is 1.42 MB and has grown on every batch and never been compacted; with the other four it is several megabytes. **A writer cannot read one of them whole, and Batch 0004 could only comply by tailing the last block.** **THE FIX IS ADDITIVE AND NOT DESTRUCTIVE: `state/live-canon.md` NOW HOLDS THE CLOSED FIGURES, THE FREE-FIGURE LIST, THE MOTIF CAPS, THE THREE DAY-COUNTS WITH THE ANCHORS AND THE NEXT BATCH'S FIGURES ALREADY ADDED UP, THE SIX OPEN QUESTIONS, THE ELEVEN STANDING REFUSALS, THE STANDING OF JONAS MERCER, THE NINE STANDING, AND THE THREE OPEN CANON QUESTIONS — AND THE BATCH 0005 PROMPT NAMES IT AS THE FIRST THING TO READ.** **THE APPEND-ONLY HISTORY IS DELIBERATELY NOT PRUNED, AND NO WRITER MAY PRUNE IT, COMPACT IT OR TRUNCATE IT:** an old block is the only record that a figure was ever spent, and cutting one loses it for good. **WHERE `state/live-canon.md` AND ANYTHING ABOVE IT DISAGREE, THE THING ABOVE IS RIGHT AND THE FILE IS WRONG AND HAS TO BE CORRECTED.** It was rebuilt after this repair and carries no claim of its own.

**REPORTED AND NOT TOUCHED, BOTH CONTROLLER-OWNED, BOTH FOR A HUMAN:**

11. **The Volume 05 boundary.** On the page Volume 05 runs 195–242 and Volume 06 begins at 243; the next batch is ten cards, 235–244. **THE WRITER FLAGGED IT IN THREE PLACES AND REFUSED TO REWRITE AN OUTLINE TO HIDE IT, WHICH WAS RIGHT.** The Batch 0005 prompt now says, in terms: if no human has decided by the time that batch is written, write all ten cards as dated, **leave 243 and 244 marked as belonging to no volume, do not write a Volume 06 outline, do not write a volume-close prompt, and do not call card 0244 a close.** The decision is still a human's.
12. **The review agent has not been running as a review agent.** The runner invokes `novel-reviewer` top-level while its definition declares it a subagent, so the model falls back to the writer and **the writer has been auditing its own output**, which is the one thing the quality gate is meant to prevent. The agent definition also denies bash, so it could not run the measuring passes the process depends on — and the defect it would most obviously have caught, the closed figure in 0231, is exactly what a measuring pass catches. **`.opencode/agent/` AND `.github/workflows/` ARE CONTROLLER FILES AND WERE NOT EDITED.** For a human.
13. **`state/phase-ledger.json` is stale.** It reports the run at phase-002-batch-plan, Volume 1 Batch 1, range 1–10, while the manuscript stands at 234, and its phase ids do not match the `workspace/` directory names. **CONTROLLER-OWNED, NOT EDITED, REPORTED ONLY.**

**AND WHAT THE REVIEW FOUND CLEAN, WHICH IS RECORDED SO IT IS NOT RE-LITIGATED:** all ten chapter weekdays are right against the real 2016 calendar and all nine gaps match the prompt; the all-caps paragraphs and the scene breaks are inside their caps; none of the closed figures is otherwise on the page; **the city is named only *Tideglass*, with no *Nacre*, no *River Stacks*, no narrated rename and nobody noticing**; the word *warden* appears nowhere; the gate sentence is used once, unstaged, in a list; the 507-day count matches the anchor with no stray day-counts; and **the reversal itself is genuinely spent through somebody doing something — two columns added in pencil on a fuel slip, twice, with the same answer both times — and not through a narrator's paragraph about a thing.** It is not fraud, it has produced no villain, it has discovered no person, it is not a twelfth refusal, and it is not resolved. Nobody died, nobody was rescued, the register holds at nineteen, and nobody was given a title, a seat or a minute.

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 254 — Saturday the twenty-third of July 2016, from about eleven in the morning, in this city

**THE BOROUGH'S FIRST CALL IS GONE, A COMPACT EXISTS THAT CANNOT BE POINTED AT, A RIGHT OF RETURN IS THE SIZE OF ONE TUESDAY, A PRICE HAS COME BACK DOWN FOR THE SAME REASON IT WENT UP, A PACT IS HOLDING BECAUSE NOBODY IS COUNTING IT, TWO SHEETS ARE IN A LOCKED DRAWER UNDER A TRADE COUNTER, A MAN IN HIS SIXTIES HAS PUT A WRONG ANSWER ABOUT THE REGISTER INTO TWO ROOMS AND A CORRIDOR ON PURPOSE, AND A QUESTION WAS SENT TO THE OTHER SIDE OF THE ESTUARY AND CAME BACK IN THE WRONG DIRECTION, AND NOBODY HAS ANSWERED ANYTHING.**

- **THE ORDINARY WEEK, WHICH IS THE FIRST TIME IN THIS ACCOUNT THAT THE SACRIFICE HAS BEEN DESCRIBED AS A WEEK INSTEAD OF AS A NIGHT.** The boat and the engine are both out of the borough on a Tuesday and neither was asked to go. The only new fact is that a van that used to come back on a Sunday now comes back when it comes back. Tomas Renn named the mechanism himself: my lot said Sunday, the Lowdale lad said Sunday, the man at the bottom house said Sunday because he'd heard the Lowdale lad say it, and none of those three had ever been asked by anybody, and that is the mechanism. Nine years it was Sunday, and it is not Sunday now and there is not a new word for it.
- **THE LOW END, ON AN EVENING, IN JUNE.** A man on a stepladder burst his own hose. The man in the doorway of number forty-one told him to get the one off the wall and gave it away. A woman at number thirty-eight was already in her own yard at seven with two washing lines full. And into the ladder, once, in one sentence, about a Tuesday and nothing else: *If your lot are out on a Tuesday, I'll come down and bring that hose out, and I don't want it on that board.* **Nobody wrote it down. Nobody asked him a question. A pump is not a rescue and a hose is not a rota row and neither sentence is said or implied anywhere in the ten chapters.**
- **THE PACT AS A THING.** A woman walked half a mile to the yard at Penhale and asked how you know it is working, and the answer took her twenty minutes to arrive at. **THE PACT HOLDS AND IT HOLDS BECAUSE NOBODY IS COUNTING IT, AND THE PROOF IS A COLUMN IN A BOOK RULED SINCE 1987 WITH A GAP IN IT ABOUT TWO INCHES DEEP, AND A LINE THAT IS NOT A CLAIM IS IN THE GAP.** The man in his forties refused a figure: he could tell you to the penny how much went through that shed last year and has never once been asked, and the day somebody writes a number like that down it stops being a fact about a yard and becomes a figure about a yard. Nine yards on that bend, about four of which have never sent a crew out and never took one in and pay in exactly the same, and there is no list on which those four are the four, because there is no list. Haldane Perry would not let the wall sheet down. **The second line under the first on that wall is still not explained by anybody and that is correct.**
- **THE PRICE, AND IT IS NOT A TRIUMPH.** The woman in the second house of Marsh Lane is back to a hundred and eighteen and would rather not be pleased about it, because it came down the day the boat came back and she had nothing to do with the boat coming back. **The drain man is back to about what he had in February and is not back.** Four streets became two and a half in May; one of the two who came in has stopped coming in, so he has two and three-quarter streets, and back would be four, and that is the part of this he does not get back. The four companies said the same thing for the fifth time and none of them added to it.
- **THE RIGHT OF RETURN, ASKED OF PERSONS.** The man of about eighteen put it to about four people in doorways, one at a time, with nothing written down, and did not make a list, and the reason is not a principle. **Two said yes and what they said yes to was a day.** A right of return in this borough is currently the size of one Tuesday: one Tuesday and a man who finishes at two, and the day after that he is in Leeds till Wednesday. It is not reciprocal and was not sold as reciprocal, and that was the argument: if you pretend it is reciprocal this becomes a thing with two halves, and a thing with two halves gets a name, and the name gets a person under it, and then it is the other thing. **The only person who had ever been reliably there was not asked, and that is arithmetic and not sentiment.**
- **THE SIX, HANDED ROUND TO PEOPLE WHO HAD TO ACT ON THEM.** The verb was changed to *what would you do about it on Monday* and the honest answer was the same one six times. **A COMPACT IS NOT A THING A ROOM CAN AGREE TO AND A QUESTION IS NOT ANSWERED BY ASKING A ROOM WHAT IT WOULD DO. NONE OF THE SIX WAS ANSWERED. AND ONE OF THEM WAS ANSWERED WRONG ON PURPOSE BY THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES, WHO SAID SO BEFORE HE PUT HIS HAND UP, AND THE ROOM DID NOT CORRECT HIM, BECAUSE YOU ARE ONLY ALLOWED TO LEAVE A WRONG THING IN A ROOM IF YOU CAN PUT THE RIGHT ONE WHERE IT WAS AND NOBODY COULD.** The wrong answer was that the register is the dated thing that will be the first one proved. It is not: the register is at nineteen, the last entry is a bulb, and everything this borough has done since is not in it because it was not written down. **By Wednesday it had got itself into a corridor by itself, by a mouth that had never heard him say it and would never know he had arranged it.**
- **THE SHEET OF EIGHT TOWNS, A SECOND TIME, AND EIGHT NAMES.** It came out of the locked drawer under the trade counter for the second time in this borough, in a handbag and out of it, and the key came in the handbag and went back in it and has never once been given to anybody in nine years, and the reason is not the paper: if somebody wanted to look at it in March I'd have said no in March. **Nobody can act on it and that is correct, and the one thing that can be done with it is not an act: eight places read out loud twice, and eight people in the room saying their own names out loud once, in no order, in whatever order they got up in.** One hand out of nine, for Ockbrook, and she could not point at it. **Nothing was written down, because there was nothing on the table to write it on and nobody had brought anything. The man at number forty-one's name is not one of the eight and nobody suggested another name.** It still carries no charter, no proxy, no root term, no hinge, no seal, no date of origin, no name of a body, no mechanism and no explanation.
- **WHAT TWO YESES AND ONE NO BUILT, WHICH IS A NEGATIVE FINDING.** Twelve pencil ticks in a book the woman with the key has kept for nine years, twelve rooms with about nine people in them at some point, and one line in a column headed with nothing at all at the bottom of a hill. **The compact is the thing Mrs Obi said it would not be, which is a list, and nobody has made one.** The only evidence it exists is that on Tuesday the twenty-eighth of June four people did something small without arranging it: a bath out of a second-floor landing in Marsh Lane, four feet of pipe left on a kerb by the boat crew, a pound in a meter put in by somebody nobody saw, and a length of chain bought by the man at number forty-one who would not say which gate it was for. **Nobody could connect the four of them, and the only thing that made them the same day is that one man said a date out loud in a street to somebody washing up in a kitchen and did not put it in a list and went back to work.**
- **THE QUESTION PUT TO THE REGION, AND THE WRONG DIRECTION.** A question went out inside somebody else's folder, on the back of the last docket in a boat's job wallet, in a hand that was not a tradesman's hand: *Who printed the first one, and are they still doing it.* No name, no address, no company, no date, no reference. What came back on the Friday is correct, and it is not a bill, and it is not a demand, and it is not a claim, and it is a sheet: the same line of printing, and under it one place, centred, on its own. **The place is this borough.** And it is not about us at all, it is about a place: it says a place is one of the places from which a thing may be used, and it does not say anybody is coming, it does not say anything is owed, and it does not say that a person who lives there has been counted or heard from or written down anywhere. **It does not answer the question. Somebody read two questions about a person and answered with a place, and that is a perfectly good answer to a question nobody asked. Nobody on either side of that water has done anything wrong or anything clever or anything hostile, and not one person has been discovered, and nobody is a perpetrator, and nothing is a fraud.** It is in the drawer under the trade counter on top of the other sheet and the lock went round twice.
- **AND ONE SMALL THING HAPPENED TO JONAS MERCER.** A man behind a till on the parade asked him what a two-inch swept bend went for in the catalogue, and he answered, and it took about four seconds, and it was correct, and the man said cheers and wrote it on the back of a receipt and did not ask him anything else. **It is the first thing anybody in this borough has asked him since the eleventh of May and it is a price. He is not restored to anything and nothing happened.**
- **AND EVERYTHING ELSE IS WHERE IT WAS.** The register is at nineteen, the nineteenth is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb and the bulb is out, and the line under it is **six hundred and five days** and is still blank. A card has been in a slot five hundred and nineteen days and has not been taken out, put up or read. A letter has been in a tray on the second floor under two other pieces of post five hundred and twenty-one days, sealed, and **no chapter of chapters 195–254 has gone near that room.** The page of 29 May 2015 is not in force and its one reader has not been asked again. The nine printed sheets of 19 June still read *no requirement stated* and have no name box. The column headed *date seen again* is still empty. The counter in the vestibule is in no scene in this batch, answered nothing and was not entered, and the drawer under it is not opened, not counted, not asked about and not mentioned. The rota is not rebuilt and the 11:00–13:30 row is still empty. The second line under Bram Osei's Thursday has not been looked at. The board is at seven of nine. The ninth seat is fifty-four and unpaid and untold. The buff file of 14 October 2011 is not opened and four people have seen the bay number and the date on that cover. Paragraph 4.11 is not answered and the compliance week is not verified. Roy Albery does not walk again. **Nobody died, nobody was rescued, nobody renewed, expired or renamed anything, nothing was minuted, no chapter of this batch contains a thanks of any kind, and the eleven standing refusals are eleven.**

**AND THE SIX OPEN QUESTIONS ARE ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE WAS ANSWERED. From 0184, which dated thing will be the first somebody can prove — and it is now the only one of the six that anybody in this borough has answered in public, wrongly, on purpose, and left standing. From 0174, which of the eight will be the first somebody decides to keep, and whether it is kept because it was good or because it was expensive — a man from the parade answered it for himself and said *that's me, not the answer*. From 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first thing a person is asked to agree to — a pad on a knee with a date on it and nothing else is still a candidate and was refused a count by two people. From 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city has to stop, and what a person has to be holding to stop each — the second of them is still nothing, and it was asked out loud in plain words and answered in two words. The volume's own, about a permission, an obligation and now a right of return — it now has a fourth document in it, a sheet with this borough's own name printed under the rule. And the sixth, about a document and not a person — none of the three of them has been opened since the fourth of June and none will be. **And the question this volume owed, asked once on the fourth of June by a man of about eighteen, was answered by nobody in these ten chapters, and on the twenty-third of July a woman with a key asked him in four words whether anybody had said anything to him about it and he said no.**

**AND FOUR FLAGS GO TO A HUMAN AND NONE OF THEM IS A WRITER'S. THE NAME OF THE CITY. WHAT VOLUME 05 IS CENTRALLY, THE SERIES OUTLINE SAYING A RIVER DISASTER AND MUTUAL-AID PERMISSIONS AND THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE PROMPT SAYING THE RECLAMATION SEASON AND EVERY OLD ROOT TERM EXPIRING IN A DIFFERENT PLACE, WITH THE OPERATIVE RULES BINDING AND BINDED AND NOTHING RESOLVED. THE BOUNDARY, WHICH IS A HUMAN'S, WITH CHAPTERS 243 THROUGH 254 BELONGING TO NO VOLUME. AND NOW A SPELLING: CHAPTERS 0244 AND 0251 PRINT *Halsey Cross*, 0243 PRINTS IT IN ITS BODY AND *Halse Cross* IN ITS OWN CLOSING BLOCK, AND FOUR STATE FILES PRINT BOTH SPELLINGS, ONE PRINTS ONLY *Halsey Cross* AND ONE ONLY *Halse Cross*, SO THE CONFLICT IS INSIDE THE STATE FILES AND NOT BETWEEN THE CHAPTERS AND THE STATE — A CHAPTER GOVERNS, A WRITER DID NOT DECIDE IT, AND A HUMAN MAY ALSO HAVE TO RULE ON 0243 AGAINST ITSELF.**


**AND A REVIEW WAS RUN OVER CHAPTERS 245–254 AFTER THIS BLOCK WAS WRITTEN, AND SIX FINDINGS WERE REPAIRED AT THE SOURCE: SIX CHAPTERS AND A SEVENTH HAD OPENING AND CLOSING ALL-CAPS BLOCKS THAT RESTATED EACH OTHER AND SEVEN CLOSING BLOCKS HAVE BEEN REWRITTEN AS CONSEQUENCE; *A TUESDAY* WAS OVER A CAP OF TWO IN FIVE CHAPTERS AND IS NOW AT OR UNDER IT IN ALL TEN; THREE CHAPTERS HAD A NARRATOR EXPLAINING A SCENE THAT HAD ALREADY SHOWN IT AND TWO PARAGRAPHS AND TWO TAILS WERE CUT; `state/chapter-summaries.md` WAS STORING FIVE CHAPTERS' OWN OPENING PARAGRAPHS VERBATIM AND ALL TEN ENTRIES HAVE BEEN REWRITTEN; ONE LINE IN `state/character-state.md` MISATTRIBUTED THE WORD *US* TO HANNA WRAY WHEN THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE SAID IT FIRST; AND AN UNANCHORED *THE DRAWER* AT THE END OF 0254 IS NOW HER BAG. NO CARD, DATE, DAY-COUNT, FIGURE, NAME, MAJOR TURN OR STANDING WAS CHANGED, EVERY CHAPTER STILL CARRIES EXACTLY TWO ALL-CAPS PARAGRAPHS, AND THE FULL RECORD IS §12A OF THE CONTINUATION 0006 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md`. NOTHING IN THE BLOCK ABOVE WAS CHANGED BY ANY OF IT.**
