## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 454 — the Saturday of the seventeenth of November 2018, from about half past three in the afternoon until about six in the evening, in this city

**WHAT THIS BOROUGH IS CARRYING AT THE END OF IT: A RUN OF THIRTY-ONE CONCRETE STEPS THAT ONE MAN HAS BEEN CLEANING EVERY WEEKDAY MORNING FOR SIX YEARS WITH NOBODY'S KNOWLEDGE; AN EMPTY ROOM AT THE TOP OF A BUILDING THAT TWO PEOPLE WANT AND NEITHER OF THEM WILL SAY SO; A KNOT THAT WAS TIED WRONG TWICE BEHIND A TERRACE; TWO PLATES A WEEK GOING UP A HALF-LANDING TO A MAN WHO EATS TWO FORKFULS; A BOX IN A HALL FOR A HOUSE NEXT DOOR; HALF A SHOP WINDOW DONE BY TWO PEOPLE WHO HAVE NEVER MENTIONED IT; A STRIP OF METAL IN A WORN TREAD; A FRONT DOOR HUNG WITH NO LOCK IN IT AND A BARE KEEP ON THE FRAME; AN ENAMELLED PLATE ON A WALL THAT NOBODY WILL EVER WRITE DOWN; A SIDE DOOR PUT BACK ON A LONGER SCREW AN INCH AND A HALF OUT; AND A RULED SPACE UNDER A NINETEENTH LINE THAT WAS OPEN FOR ONE THOUSAND FOUR HUNDRED AND FIFTY-TWO DAYS WHEN THE AFTERNOON CAME. NOTHING WAS RESOLVED AND NOBODY WAS ASKED ABOUT ANY OF IT.**

- **THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES DID SEVEN THINGS IN THE FORTY-FOUR DAYS FROM THE FOURTH OF OCTOBER TO THE SEVENTEENTH OF NOVEMBER AND TOLD NOBODY ABOUT ANY OF THEM.** He found a rectangle of steps that was already clean and could not account for it and went back the next morning and did the run from the bottom the way he always does. He said four words to a woman on a landing and was told to mind her own. He would not carry a box round to the house it was for and would not give it back. He had been wiping half a shop window for six years and stopped in the street for two seconds when somebody else started the other half. He hung a door he could not finish and was paid the full figure. He agreed with another man about a plate on a wall and wrote nothing down. He had gone up that parade earlier in the afternoon of the Saturday and has not been near that side door at any time since. **HE WAS TOLD ONE TRUE THING IN THOSE FORTY-FOUR DAYS AND COULD NOT ACT ON IT AND HAS TOLD NOBODY ANYTHING.**

- **THE MAN IN HIS THIRTIES DID SIX THINGS AND SPOKE TO STRANGERS TWICE AND SAID NINE WORDS OUTRIGHT ONCE.** He stood in a room at the top of a building and said it was a good room and worked out on a roof what he had not been told. He watched a woman get a knot wrong twice from behind his own van and had six seconds in which to say one word and said nothing both times. He stopped where he was for the best part of two seconds and went on with his own half. He said a true thing about a worn tread in a doorway and went out into the parade, and four days later saw the result of it from two hundred yard off and went round by the arch. He agreed with the man in his forties about a plate and wrote nothing down. He put his shoulder into a side door that came off its top hinge and helped get it hung on a screw he did not fit. **HE IS ON NOTHING, NOBODY HAS GIVEN HIM ANYTHING TO PUT A NAME ON, AND HE HAS NOT SAID OUT LOUD IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS THAT HE WILL DO A THING AND NOT DONE IT.**

- **THE WOMAN WHO WORKS IN A SHOP DID FIVE THINGS AND WAS TOLD ONE TRUE THING SHE COULD NOT ACT ON.** She asked one question about an empty room and not a second. She was told there was no point in a plate of food and told a man to mind his own and has been angry with herself about it about once a day since. She started wiping the other half of a shop window at ten past seven. She paid about thirty pounds of her own money for a nosing in a worn stair tread four days after a man said nine words in her doorway. She came out of her own doorway, looked at a door that had come off its top hinge, and went back in. **SHE HAS TOLD NOBODY AND IS NOT GOING TO, AND NOBODY HAS THANKED HER AND NOBODY IS GOING TO ASK HER WHY SHE DID NOT MENTION IT.**

- **THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES ASKED ONE QUESTION AND GOT HALF AN ANSWER AND CROSSED A PARADE WITH A BAG OF TOOLS.** He asked who was doing the window, was told one half of it, said it was good for it, and has never mentioned the other half since. On the Saturday afternoon he put his cup down on the pavement when he saw two men near a door and then went and pulled a plate and a screw and about three inches of brick out of a wall and put a longer screw into sound brick. **HE HAS BEEN ASKED NOTHING IN EITHER OF THOSE TWO AND WILL NOT BE ASKED, AND HE DOES NOT KNOW THAT HE AND THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES HAVE BOTH BEEN DOING HALF OF SOMETHING FOR SIX YEARS WITHOUT EVER HAVING SPOKEN ABOUT IT.**

- **AND SIX PEOPLE WERE ON A PARADE ON A SATURDAY AFTERNOON FOR PRIVATE REASONS AND A SEVENTH WENT UP AT THE TOP END WITH A KEY AND CAME BACK WITH A BROOM, AND NOT ONE OF THE SEVEN KNEW ABOUT ANY OF THE OTHER SIX.** A woman with a dog came past at about four. A man who works nights went down the other way. A woman with a pram said one word and wheeled off. Nine people were out in all and none of them was there because of any of the other eight.

- **AND A MAN PUT A PLATE BACK ON A WALL IN FRONT OF TWO MEN ABOUT FIVE YEARS AGO AND SAID NOTHING WHILE HE DID IT, AND NEITHER OF THOSE TWO MEN KNEW IT UNTIL THE OTHER ONE SAID IT ON A PAVEMENT IN NOVEMBER, AND NEITHER OF THEM HAS SAID A WORD ABOUT IT TO ANYBODY ELSE, AND THE PLATE ON THE WALL IS A DIFFERENT PLATE.**

- **AND IN A ROOM IN A BLOCK BY THAT WATER, BEHIND A PLACE THAT HAS NEVER CAUGHT, A BOOK STANDS OPEN ON A SHELF.** Nobody has added a twentieth line to it. **THE SPACE UNDER THAT LAST LINE had been open across one thousand four hundred and fifty-two days WHEN THE SEVENTEENTH OF NOVEMBER CAME, AND NOBODY PUT A FIGURE ON IT AND NOBODY WENT IN AND NOBODY COUNTED IT.**

- **AND FOUR ANSWERS TO ONE QUESTION ARE STILL GOING ROUND, AND NONE OF THEM CAN BE PUT BESIDE ANY OTHER.** There is no fifth, none of the four has been taken back, and nobody has said what any of them is an answer to, and nobody was asked one on that Saturday. It is still a question, and it is a question nobody here is putting to anybody else.

- **AND THERE IS STILL SOMETHING IN THIS BOROUGH THAT NOT ONE PERSON LIVING IN IT COULD NAME IF YOU STOPPED THEM IN THE STREET, AND IT IS THE SAME SOMETHING IT WAS AT THE END OF AUGUST, AND IT HAS NOT ALTERED BY SO MUCH AS A DAY, AND NOBODY HAS MENTIONED IT.**

- **AND THE BOROUGH IS ORDINARY AND THE FORTY-FOUR DAYS FROM THE FOURTH OF OCTOBER WERE THE SAME KIND OF DAYS AS THE ONES BEFORE THEM AND NOT A TURN OF ANYTHING.** Steps were cleaned and one of them was already clean · a room was wanted and not asked for · a knot ran twice and was not corrected · two plates went up a landing twice a week · a box came to the wrong door and stayed · half a window was done by two people · a nosing was fitted in a tread and neither of them mentioned it · a door was hung without its lock and paid for as done · a plate was agreed about and written nowhere · and a side door came off its top hinge and was put back on the wrong screw and told to nobody.

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 464 — the Thursday of the third of January 2019, from about a quarter to twelve until about ten past two, in this city

**WHAT THIS BOROUGH IS CARRYING AT THE END OF IT: NINE FOOT of hard standing with a wall along one side of it THAT TWO PEOPLE BOTH WANT AND NEITHER TOOK; A STORE DOOR A MAN SHUT ON A DRAUGHT AND A RING IN THE DUST ON A BOX THAT HE HAS TOLD NOBODY ABOUT; A GATE A WOMAN NO LONGER STANDS AT AND SIX MINUTES SHE NOW WALKS EVERY MORNING; A DOOR AT THE BOTTOM OF A PASSAGE THAT TWO PEOPLE CHANGED FOURTEEN DAYS APART; A TRUE ANSWER GIVEN IN A PASSAGE THAT CANNOT BE UNSAID; A BUILDING THAT IS NOT EMPTY AND A WOMAN ACROSS THE STREET WHO HAS PROVED IT AND SAID NOTHING; A PIECE OF WIRE IN A JOINT PUT IN TWICE BY TWO MEN WHO HAVE NEVER MET; A LENGTH OF WALL WITH A FOOT OF IT LEFT; A STEP OVER RUNNING WATER THAT A WOMAN KEEPS CROSSING WRONG; AND A RULED SPACE UNDER A NINETEENTH LINE THAT HAD BEEN OPEN since the middle of March three years before that WHEN THAT THURSDAY CAME. NOTHING WAS RESOLVED AND NOBODY WAS ASKED ABOUT ANY OF IT.**

- **THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES DID FIVE THINGS IN THE FORTY-FOUR DAYS FROM THE TWENTIETH OF NOVEMBER TO THE THIRD OF JANUARY AND TOLD NOBODY ABOUT ANY OF THEM.** He wanted that ground and could not bring himself to take it. He shut a door that was moving and found a mark in the dust and has carried it since. He bent a wire into a joint in a path at twenty past eight one Monday morning. He told a man with a docket that a length of wall was away and left the foot of it unpointed. And he was the first to a wall at half past twelve on that Thursday and does not know the woman with the dog said one word about it. **HE HAS TOLD NOBODY ANYTHING.**

- **THE MAN IN HIS THIRTIES DID THREE THINGS AND SAID SEVEN WORDS OUTRIGHT.** He stopped taking a door through with him, fourteen days after a woman held it for him once. In December he bent wire into a joint in that path on a Wednesday evening and has spent every day since working out who had done it first. And he crossed a step over running water without looking up, and a woman behind him did it once and got it wrong. **HE IS ON NOTHING, NOBODY HAS GIVEN HIM ANYTHING TO PUT A NAME ON, AND HE HAS NOT SAID OUT LOUD IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS THAT HE WILL DO A THING AND NOT DONE IT.**

- **THE WOMAN WHO HAS THE GROUND FLOOR OF THAT PASSAGE DID TWO THINGS AND WAS TOLD ONE TRUE THING SHE COULD NOT ACT ON.** She held a door for a man, which she had never done, and a fortnight later she let it go for the first time and did not go back. And on the tenth of December she asked a man in a passage whether anybody else was in that building at night, and was answered truthfully, and has not asked him one further thing and does not intend to. **SHE HAS TOLD NOBODY AND IS NOT GOING TO.**

- **THE WOMAN WHO WALKED UP THAT STREET TO WORK FOR NINE YEARS DID ONE THING FOR A FORTNIGHT AND NOBODY NOTICED IT.** She altered her route on the fifteenth of November, about a hundred and thirty yard and six minutes, after working out that a woman she has never spoken to was no longer where she had been used to seeing her. Between four and nine people watched her do it every morning and not one said a word to her about any of it. **SHE WILL NOT OPEN THE SUBJECT HERSELF.**

- **THE WOMAN ACROSS FROM NUMBER NINE DID ONE THING FOR SEVEN WEEKS AND TOLD NOBODY.** She established from four things she could see and hear that the top two floors of that building are not empty, worked out that the man with the cup knows it too, and has said nothing to him, to the woman at number eight, or to anybody. **THE BUILDING IS NOT CHANGED BY ANY OF IT.**

- **THE woman on the first floor of the nearer of those two blocks DID ONE THING AND SAID NOTHING.** She found a slab lifted out of a path on the Tuesday, stood over the hole about a minute, and went inside. She has kept it to herself and is going to go on keeping it. **SHE HAS WATCHED A MAN PUT A WIRE IN THAT JOINT FOUR DAYS BEFORE HE DID AND DOES NOT KNOW IT.**

- **AND SIX PEOPLE WERE ON A PARADE ON A THURSDAY MORNING FOR PRIVATE REASONS AND NOT ONE OF THEM KNEW ABOUT ANY OF THE OTHER FIVE.** A woman with a dog came past at about twenty past twelve and again at ten past two. A man who puts shutters up came down at ten to twelve and went back. Down that parade at about ten past one went the man who works nights, and a girl of about sixteen went up at twelve and came back at two with nothing. A woman with a bag went round a length of iron lying on a footway. A woman with a dog said one word and went on. **A MAN IN HIS THIRTIES WALKED DOWN THAT PARADE AT TWENTY TO ONE AND A MAN IN HIS FORTIES WALKED UP IT WITH A BROWN PAPER BAG AND NEITHER OF THEM LOOKED AT THE WALL.**

- **AND IN A ROOM AT THE END OF A PASSAGE IN A BUILDING BY THAT WATER, A BOOK STANDS OPEN ON A SHELF.** Nobody has added a twentieth line to it. **THE SPACE UNDER THAT LAST LINE had been open across one thousand four hundred and ninety-nine days WHEN THE THIRD OF JANUARY CAME, AND NOBODY PUT A FIGURE ON IT AND NOBODY WENT IN AND NOBODY COUNTED IT.**

- **AND FOUR ANSWERS TO ONE QUESTION ARE STILL GOING ROUND, AND NONE OF THEM CAN BE PUT BESIDE ANY OTHER.** There is no fifth, none of the four has been taken back, and nobody has said what any of them is an answer to, and nobody was asked one on that Thursday. It is still a question, and it is a question nobody here is putting to anybody else.

- **AND THERE IS STILL SOMETHING IN THIS BOROUGH THAT NOT ONE PERSON LIVING IN IT COULD NAME IF YOU STOPPED THEM IN THE STREET, AND IT IS THE SAME SOMETHING IT WAS AT THE END OF AUGUST, AND IT HAS NOT ALTERED BY SO MUCH AS A DAY, AND NOBODY HAS MENTIONED IT.**

- **AND THE BOROUGH IS ORDINARY AND THE FORTY-FOUR DAYS FROM THE TWENTIETH OF NOVEMBER WERE THE SAME KIND OF DAYS AS THE ONES BEFORE THEM AND NOT A TURN OF ANYTHING, AND THE SCHEDULE CROSSING THE TURN OF THE YEAR IS NOT A SEASON AND NOT A TURN.** Nine foot of ground was wanted by two people and taken by neither · a store door was shut and a ring in the dust was seen by one man and told to nobody · A woman altered her route for a fortnight · a door was held and then let go and neither of them mentioned it · a man gave a true answer to the wrong question and cannot take it back · a building was proved not empty and said nothing · a piece of wire went into a joint twice · a wall was reported away with A FOOT OF IT LEFT · A woman watched a man cross running water and did it wrong and will do it again · and something came off a wall and was put back out of line and told to nobody.

- **AND EVERYTHING ELSE IS WHERE IT WAS.** A piece of paper has been in a slot one thousand four hundred and thirteen days and has not been taken out, put up or read, and it is in no chapter of this batch. A letter has been in a tray on the second floor under two other pieces of post one thousand four hundred and fifteen days, sealed, and **no chapter of chapters 455–464 went near that room.** The page of 29 May 2015 is not in force and its one reader has not been asked again. The nine printed sheets of 19 June still read *no requirement stated* and have no name box. The column headed *date seen again* is still empty. The counter in the vestibule is in no scene in this batch, answered nothing and was not entered, and **nothing under it is opened, not counted, not asked about and not mentioned, and the word *drawer* is at zero in all ten chapters, as is the word *envelope*.** The rota is not rebuilt and the 11:00–13:30 row is not mentioned. The second line under Bram Osei's Thursday has not been looked at. The board is at seven of nine. The ninth seat is fifty-four and unpaid and untold. The buff file of 14 October 2011 is not opened and four people have seen the bay number and the date on that cover. Paragraph 4.11 is not answered and the compliance week is not verified. Roy Albery does not walk again. **Nobody died, nobody was rescued, nobody was housed by anybody, nobody renewed, expired or renamed anything, nothing was minuted, no chapter of this batch contains a thanks of any kind, the word *thank* is at zero in all ten on word boundaries and on the substring, the word *eleven* is at zero, the word *forty* is at zero, the word *hall* is at zero, and the stem *drain-* is at zero.**

- **AND THE SIX OPEN QUESTIONS ARE ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE WAS ANSWERED. From 0184, which dated thing will be the first somebody can prove — **still open, and it went the same way for the sixteenth batch in a row, and the four answers cannot be put beside one another, and a fifth has not joined them and is not going to. From 0174, which of the eight will be the first somebody decides to keep — **untouched. From 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first thing a person is asked to agree to — **untouched, and nine foot of ground was wanted and not asked for, a man was asked a question in a passage and answered it, a door was changed by two people who did not discuss it, a length of iron came off a wall and was put back, and not one of the four was asked about. From 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city has to stop, and what a person has to be holding to stop each — **still open, and the second of them is still nothing. The volume's own, about a permission, an obligation and a right of return — **untouched, and the word *permission* is at zero in all ten chapters. And the sixth, about a document and not a person — **no piece of paper that leaves a building was created in this batch, and the things that were put down, carried, shut, opened, dropped, twisted, lifted, put back and put right were four trestles, eight pieces of a removal, a bag of mortar, a brown paper bag, a trowel, a piece of wire, a length of iron and a ruled space under a nineteenth line, and nobody has a copy of any of it.**

- **AND SEVEN FLAGS GO TO A HUMAN AND NONE OF THEM IS A WRITER'S. THE NAME OF THE CITY, WHICH THE PROSE CALLS TIDEGLASS, AND *NACRE* AND *RIVER STACKS* AND EVERY DISTRICT NAME APPEAR NOWHERE IN CHAPTERS 455–464 BUT ONCE IN EACH DATELINE. THE SEVENTH, **THE THIRD SCRIPT FAULT IN TWO BLOCKS, AT §12C ITEM 3 OF THAT BLOCK, WHICH IS THAT THE INHERITED RUN SEVEN FUNCTION SCANNED THE WHOLE TEXT OF THE TEN CHAPTERS INSTEAD OF THE TEN DATELINES AND REPORTED SEVEN REUSES UNDER A HEADING THAT SAID IT WAS MEASURING DATELINES, AND THAT A LATER WRITER MUST CHECK WHAT ITS OWN FUNCTION READS BEFORE IT BELIEVES IT.**

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 474 — the Sunday of the twenty-fourth of February 2019, from about twenty past ten in the morning until about half past one, in this city

**WHAT THIS BOROUGH IS CARRYING AT THE END OF IT: A COPING AT THE END OF A CUT WITH NOTHING ON IT · A SOUND AT HALF PAST TWO IN THE MORNING THAT TWO PEOPLE IN ONE BUILDING HAVE EACH EXPLAINED DIFFERENTLY AND NEITHER WILL ASK ABOUT · TWO MINUTES OF A NOISE AT HALF PAST FIVE THAT A MAN WAS ASKED ONCE TO STOP AND DID NOT · A LANDING AND A SIDE A WOMAN HAS STOOD ON SINCE 2013 BECAUSE OF SOMETHING SHE DID IN 2013 · AN ARMCHAIR ON A KERB SINCE THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF JANUARY · A HAND FLAT ON A CAR DOOR AND TWO MINUTES, AND A WINDOW THAT WAS ALREADY THERE · A MACHINE TURNED OFF AT THE BACK OF A BUILDING BY A MAN WHO WANTED NOTHING · A BOUNDARY WALL CARRYING A HOUSE NEXT DOOR · TEN SECONDS AT A CORNER IN JANUARY · FOUR FOOT OF PAVEMENT OF A DIFFERENT COLOUR · AND A RULED SPACE UNDER A NINETEENTH LINE THAT HAD BEEN OPEN FOR ONE THOUSAND FIVE HUNDRED AND FIFTY-ONE DAYS. NOTHING WAS RESOLVED AND NOBODY WAS ASKED ABOUT ANY OF IT.**

- **THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES DID ONE THING IN THESE FIFTY-TWO DAYS.** He walked from his own door to his van making a noise for about two minutes every weekday morning, was asked once by a woman above a garage behind his house to stop, stopped for a week, and then did not. He knows she has a removal booked for April and has told himself a thing about April that he is not going to say. **HE HAS TOLD NOBODY WHY HE STARTED AGAIN.**
- **THE MAN IN HIS THIRTIES DID FOUR THINGS AND SAID ONE SENTENCE THAT WAS ABOUT A WALL.** He went up a stair at twenty minutes to three in the morning and found out what a woman on the top floor of his building is doing at that hour. He put a tape against a boundary wall on the eighth of February and wrote down what he measured at the back of a book, and it came to the same as what he had in September. He had been in that wall twice, in 2013 and 2016, and he was paid nothing and told nobody. He is right about the wall and he cannot put it in front of anybody in a shape anybody would take. **HE HAS NOT SAID IT AGAIN SINCE THE SEVENTH OF SEPTEMBER AND WILL NOT.**
- **THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES IS IN TWO CHAPTERS AND IS NOT AGED IN EITHER.** In January a woman on the third floor of a four-floors-to-a-floor building told him, kindly, that the ear she had been standing next to for six years is the one that hears everything. He put his parcel down, said it without any edge on it whatever, added that he could hear her in 2013 as well, and went up two steps. **HE HAS BEEN ASKED NOTHING AND WILL NOT BE.**
- **A MAN AT A CORNER OF A STREET HAS WORKED OUT WHAT A WOMAN DID ONCE IN JANUARY AND CANNOT ASK HER, AND HAS TOLD NOBODY, AND IS NOT GOING TO.** She has made up her mind not to learn whether he understood it, because one of the two things that could be true costs her nothing at all and the other one costs her a conversation she is not equipped for.
- **THE WOMAN WHO HAS THE CAT HAS HAD IT BEHIND A SHUT DOOR SINCE BEFORE CHRISTMAS, HAS SEEN IT TWICE IN A GARDEN FROM HER OWN WINDOW, AND HAS NOT GONE OUT OF HER DOOR.** A man in another street has been going round a wall for a fortnight and then went through it, and neither of them knows the other is in it.
- **THE WOMAN ABOVE THE GARAGE SAID ONE SENTENCE AT HALF PAST FIVE IN THE MORNING AND HAS SAID NOTHING SINCE, AND HE KNOWS SHE HAS NOT.**
- **THE WOMAN WHO CAME DOWN A STAIR AT HALF PAST EIGHT ON A SATURDAY MORNING ASKED A MAN ON THE GROUND FLOOR WHETHER ANYTHING HAD COME FOR HER AND SAID NOTHING ELSE.** Upstairs a woman left that building at twenty minutes to three in the morning carrying her shoes in her hand and neither of the two who saw it will put what they know to her, and she is awake on the other side of that ceiling.
- **A WOMAN ON THE GROUND FLOOR, SECOND DOOR ALONG, HAS CARRIED AN ARMCHAIR IN AT ONE EVERY DAY SINCE 2013 AND ON THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF JANUARY CAME OUT AND DID NOT TOUCH IT.** The man who carries it out has parked on the far side of that road ever since and has no reason he would say out loud.
- **A WOMAN FOUR HOUSES UP A STREET HAS BEEN SITTING AT A WINDOW FOR SIX YEARS WITHOUT KNOWING SHE WAS WATCHING A MAN, AND HE HAS KNOWN FOR THIRTEEN DAYS, AND NEITHER HAS RAISED IT.** She will not stop, because if she stops he will know exactly what it means.
- **TWO WOMEN, ONE ABOVE THE OTHER, HAVE EACH WANTED THAT NOISE TO STOP AND NEITHER SAID SO, AND SOMEBODY WITH A UNIT AND A TOOLBOX CHANGED ITS TIMER ON THE TWENTY-NINTH OF JANUARY, AND BOTH OF THEM WORKED IT OUT INSIDE A FORTNIGHT.** The woman on the first floor is worse off than she was in December. Neither will ask him why and neither will own to the other that she had hoped for it.
- **A WOMAN WITH A KITCHEN AGAINST THAT BOUNDARY ADDED UP EVERY ROUTE SHE HAS TO A WALL OF HER OWN AND CANNOT AFFORD ONE OF THEM, AND HAS DECIDED NOT TO ASK THE MAN WHO TOLD HER, BECAUSE ASKING HIM WOULD BE ASKING HIM TO BE SOMEBODY HE HAS TOLD HER HE IS NOT.**
- **AND NINE PEOPLE WERE ON A PARADE ON A SUNDAY MORNING FOR PRIVATE REASONS AND NOT ONE OF THEM KNEW ABOUT ANY OF THE OTHER EIGHT.** The shutter man came down off the top end and went straight back up it. A woman with a dog came by and went back the same way. The man who works nights went up and came down again. A woman carrying a bag had to step around a man who was planted in the middle of that street and doing nothing at all. A man in his sixties crossed with a cup in his hand. A man in his forties was on his way up and going out of the top. A man in his thirties was on his way down and left by the bottom end. The woman who shuts a shop at half past four stayed on her own pavement for two and a half hours and went inside for about four minutes. **A man cleared nine foot of pavement an hour before his usual hour and found four foot of a different stone under it, and nobody on that parade turned towards it.**

- **AND IN A ROOM AT THE END OF A PASSAGE IN A BUILDING BY THAT WATER, A BOOK LIES OPEN ON A SHELF WITH A PAGE PINNED IN PLACE BY A THUMB THAT WENT AWONG LONG AGO.** Nobody has added a twentieth line to it. **THE GAP UNDER THE LAST LINE had been there across one thousand five hundred and fifty-one days when the twenty-fourth of February came, and nobody counted it and nobody went down that passage.**

- **AND THERE ARE STILL FOUR ANSWERS TO ONE QUESTION IN THIS BOROUGH AND NOT TWO OF THEM WILL SIT TOGETHER.** No fifth has joined them, none has been withdrawn, no person has told another which of the four is theirs, and nobody on that parade set two of them next to each other or asked anybody what any of them was for. It is still a question, and nobody is putting it to anybody.

- **AND THERE IS STILL SOMETHING IN THIS BOROUGH THAT NOT ONE PERSON LIVING IN IT COULD NAME IF YOU STOPPED THEM IN THE STREET, AND IT IS THE SAME SOMETHING IT WAS AT THE END OF AUGUST, AND IT HAS NOT ALTERED BY SO MUCH AS A DAY, AND WHAT HAS HAPPENED TO IT SINCE IS THAT THERE IS NOBODY LEFT HERE WITH ANY REASON TO GO AND LOOK FOR IT.**

- **AND THE BOROUGH IS ORDINARY AND THE FIFTY-TWO DAYS FROM THE THIRD OF JANUARY WERE THE SAME KIND OF DAYS AS THE ONES BEFORE THEM AND NOT A TURN OF ANYTHING.** A cat was shut in · a man came down a stair at twenty to three · a woman opened a door at half past five and asked a man once to stop · a man said on a landing that he had heard her for six years · an armchair was left on a kerb · a man stood at his car for two minutes with a woman at a window · a machine was turned off by a third party · a man put two figures in a book and said nothing · a woman let a man to a kerb once · and a man swept nine foot of pavement an hour early in the rain.

- **AND EVERYTHING ELSE IS WHERE IT WAS.** A piece of paper has been in a slot one thousand four hundred and sixty-five days and has not been taken out, put up or read, and it is in no chapter of this batch. ## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 494 — the last day of this batch, an early morning and then three hours, in this city

- **THE TEN CHAPTERS AND WHAT THEY DID.** Ten chapters, 485 to 494, from Saturday the thirteenth of April 2019 to Monday the twenty-seventh of May 2019. All ten weekdays were derived from the real 2019 calendar and not carried back from 2018, and all seven of the days are used, three of them twice. The gaps, counting the incoming one, are 3, 6, 4, 5, 3, 7, 5, 4, 6 and 4 days. No chapter is dated before the floor, no two share a date, and none falls on the ninth or the eleventh of any month. Word counts 2,265 / 1,539 / 1,886 / 2,080 / 1,533 / 1,788 / 1,734 / 1,448 / 1,842 / 1,414, total 17,529 by `wc -w` on each file whole, and every figure was taken from the chapter files after every repair and not carried forward from a summary. The figures that bind are at §0 and §13A of the 0030 block in `state/continuity.md`.

- **WHAT PEOPLE ARE CARRYING, AND NONE OF IT WAS RESOLVED.** Two children each worked out the same week that their mother has given a key to a man who is in her house most days, and each wanted the other to raise it, and neither did, and neither knows the other was carrying it. A man who has gone up a car park on a Wednesday night for thirty years was asked about it for the first time and gave an answer that was a description of himself rather than a reason, and has now missed a Wednesday and then stood at the foot of a ramp without going up. Two people in a house where somebody came in and took nothing were each told a week apart by a different person, and each has decided the other does not need to know, and he cannot rule himself out. Two men in a row have not spoken about money since three seconds in a field, each believing the other has forgotten. A man took turf off a woman's lawn in 2011 and has known since January what it has cost her and has said none of it, and she does not know that anybody owes her anything and has nobody to mention it to. A man who locked up a building has given four people four different accounts of two words he said in a room in 2016 and is the only person alive who knows there are four. Two women on one side of a street have each carried a lorry stopping in the same place for three years and have never put that morning to each other, and in May each of them saw it happen again and neither went out. A man has put a bowl on a back step every night since 2013 and a woman four doors away did the same for five years and stopped in November for a reason she has told nobody and he has never asked. And a man on a round put a box inside the wrong front door in four minutes and the woman of that house carried it back herself, and neither has said a word about it to anybody.

- **THE BOROUGH HELD.** Ordinary time held. A door in an end wall stands on the latch about half the time and about nine people a day go up a stair behind it and nobody in the borough can give an account of it, and nobody has ever wanted to sit down down there. The four accounts of one thing are still in circulation, none of them can be put beside any other, a fifth has not arrived, nobody has said which of the four is theirs, and on the twenty-seventh of May nobody put the question underneath them to anybody. The line under the last entry of a working book in a room off a passage by that water is still empty, and on that date it had been empty for 1,643 days, printed once, in the body, on its own chapter's own date, and nobody counted it and nobody went down that passage. The one thing this borough still cannot put a name to has not moved since the middle of August last year, and every person who could have gone out and looked for it has been out and looked and come back in. Six weeks and two days turned nothing and was not a turn.

- **AND NOTHING ELSE MOVED.** The order is not in force and was not brought back to life, and not one word of its seven clauses or its page two was altered, and it is not mentioned by name in any chapter of this batch. The eleven standing refusals are eleven. The empty square is still empty. Nobody died, nobody was rescued, nobody was housed by anybody, nobody renewed, expired or renamed anything, nothing was minuted, not one person was discovered, and no chapter of this batch contains a thanks of any kind. **The word *thank* is at zero in all ten on word boundaries and on the substring, the word *eleven* is at zero, the word *forty* is at zero, the word *hall* is at zero, the word *chair* is at zero, the stem *drain-* is at zero, and the stems *requir-* and *permit-* are at zero.** Twenty all-caps paragraphs, two in every chapter, against a cap of five. **Eighteen scene breaks, two in eight of the ten and one in 0486 and one in 0492, against a cap of two in each** — the cap is a cap and not a target, and the two chapters that now stand at one do so because nothing changes place or hour across the join. The counter in the vestibule is in no scene in this batch, answered nothing and was not entered, and nothing under it is opened, not counted, not asked about and not mentioned. The compartment under the trade counter is a different one, was not opened, and is in no chapter. The four key-holding women are four separate people and no two of them were put in one sentence. The rota is not rebuilt and the 11:00–13:30 row is not mentioned. The second line under Bram Osei's Thursday has not been looked at. The board is at seven of nine. The ninth seat is fifty-four and unpaid and untold. The buff file of 14 October 2011 is not opened and four people have seen the bay number and the date on that cover. Paragraph 4.11 is not answered and the compliance week is not verified. A card in a slot is at 1,557 days and a letter in a tray on the second floor is at 1,559 days, and no chapter in chapters 485–494 went near that room. Roy Albery does not walk again. The upright vacuum cleaner is in that corridor and not on any van, and none of these ten chapters mentions it, which is correct and is not a finding.

- **ALL SIX OPEN QUESTIONS ARE STILL OPEN AND NONE WAS ANSWERED.** From 0184, which dated thing will be the first somebody can prove — **still open, and the four accounts cannot be put beside one another, and a fifth has not joined them, and nobody on that parade put any two of them together on the twenty-seventh of May.** From 0174, which of the eight will be the first somebody decides to keep — **untouched.** From 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first thing a person is asked to agree to — **untouched, and four people heard one sentence four ways in a room in 2016 and a fifth thing of four was told a fourth time in May, and nothing was agreed with anybody.** From 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city has to stop, and what a person has to be holding to stop each — **still open, and the second of them is still nothing.** The volume's own, about a permission, an obligation and a right of return — **untouched, and the word *permission* is at zero in all ten chapters.** And the sixth, about a document and not a person — **no piece of paper that leaves a building was created in this batch and no document, form, letter or claim appears in any of the ten, and the things that were put down, carried, built, cut, asked for, stopped, put away and put back were a bowl, a barrier, four cones, a chain, a cart, a wheel, a box, a horse's head and thirty yard of lawn, and nobody has a copy of any of it.**

- **AND WHAT THE REVIEW OF THIS BATCH FOUND, WHICH WAS NOTHING A MEASURING PASS IN THIS REPOSITORY CAN SEE.** Six findings, all repaired in the chapters or in the files, and none of them was a date, an event, a figure, a standing or a major turn. **A single ordinary word had become the batch's filler, at 113 uses across the ten chapters, and is now at 44, and the chapter that built a title on it now builds that title on a different number. Two chapters three apart turned on the same kind of door, which no window in this book compares, and one of them now turns on a door it already had in its own body, which also cleared a fault between that chapter's own opening block and its own body. A figure in the account's recurring referent had drifted a fortnight within the same month, and the chapter already on the page was kept and the later one was brought back to it. A word that had been at zero for three blocks came back four times in one chapter and is at zero in all ten, and no chapter that needed one went and got one. Ten of ten chapters ended on the same shape and two of them now do not. And two durations disagreed with the datelines of the chapters they sat in, one by six years and one by one, and both are now what those chapters' own dates make them. The full record is at §12C item 8 of the 0030 block in `state/continuity.md`.

- **AND THE MEASURING, WHICH A LATER WRITER MAY CHECK RATHER THAN ASSUME.** The seven runs at nine tokens read **RUN ONE 0/0/0, RUN TWO 0/0/0, RUN THREE 0 reuses in all four named windows, RUN FOUR 0/0/0 at the end, RUN FIVE 0/0/0, AND RUN SEVEN 0/0/0** with the driver printing all ten datelines and asserting one per file before it reported. **THE 385–394 WINDOW IS 0 RAW, 0 REUSES, LONGEST 0, AND IT HAS NOW HELD FOR ELEVEN BLOCKS RUNNING.** The three controls answered: the two state files against each other at a longest of 326 tokens, the ten against the ten immediately above at 1 RAW / 0 REUSES / LONGEST 0, and a fourteen-token plant at exactly one span of fourteen. **AND THE FIGURE THAT MATTERS MOST IS NOT ONE OF THE SEVEN: RUN FOUR CAME BACK AT 47 RAW AND 47 REUSES ON A LONGEST OF FORTY-SIX THE SECOND TIME IT WAS TAKEN, AFTER THE STATE FILES WERE WRITTEN, WHICH IS THE BIGGEST SECOND TELLING IN THIS REPOSITORY, AND EVERY ONE OF THE FORTY-SEVEN WAS THE CHARACTER-STATE ROSTER DESCRIBING FIGURES IN THE CHAPTERS' OWN WORDS. IT WAS CLEARED BY THROWING THE WHOLE NARRATIVE ROSTER OUT AND WRITING A DISPOSITION ROSTER INSTEAD — WHO, WHAT THEY ARE CARRYING, WHAT IS BARRED — WITH NOT ONE WORD OF ANY CHAPTER ALTERED.  — the Wednesday of the tenth of April 2019, from about ten past four in the afternoon until about seven in the evening, in this city

**WHAT THIS BOROUGH IS CARRYING AT THE END OF IT: FOUR YARD OF PRIVET CLOSED OVER A HUNDRED-YARD WAY AND TWO PEOPLE WHO EACH WORKED OUT THE OTHER SHOULD CUT IT · A HUNDRED YARD OF ROAD WALKED THE WRONG WAY WITH A DOG ON THE OFFSIDE, AND A MAN WHO IS NOT GOING TO BE THE SORT OF PERSON WHO SAYS THINGS · NINE SETS OF SAWN MARKS AND TWO UNDER THEM, AND TWO PEOPLE EACH RIGHT ABOUT A DIFFERENT SET · A FUCHSIA IN A PLASTIC POT CARRIED IN OUT OF RAIN, AND ONE SENTENCE A MAN USES ON EVERYTHING IN THAT HOUSE · FOUR MILES EVERY SUNDAY SINCE 1999, TWO HOURS IN A SIDE STREET, A WEEK NOT DRIVEN · A BOARDED BUILDING AND THREE OPENINGS ON A TOP FLOOR NOBODY CAN NAME · SIX FOOT OF WALL, AN HOUR, AND TWO MEN WHO EACH THOUGHT THE OTHER HAD STOPPED TRUSTING HIM · A TRUE THING SAID IN A MARKET IN FEBRUARY AND A MAN WHO STOPPED COMING ON THE FIRST OF APRIL, FOURTEEN YARD FROM A WOMAN WHO CANNOT ASK WHY · AN ACRE AND A BIT BEHIND A ROW OF TWELVE AND THIRTY SQUARE YARD AT THE TOP OF IT, AND TWO PEOPLE ASKING FOR IT WHO NEVER MET OVER IT · A CONCRETE PLANTER HALF AN INCH PROUD AND A MARK ON A SHRUB · A DOOR IN A FLANK WALL STANDING OPEN TWO INCHES, OPENING ON A ROOM NOBODY HAS BEEN INSIDE IN ABOUT EIGHT YEARS · THE FOUR ACCOUNTS OF ONE THING AND THE QUESTION BEHIND THEM · AND A RULED SPACE UNDER A NINETEENTH LINE THAT HAD BEEN OPEN FOR ONE THOUSAND FIVE HUNDRED AND NINETY-SIX DAYS. NOTHING WAS RESOLVED AND NOBODY WAS ASKED ABOUT ANY OF IT.**

- **A WOMAN SAID ONE TRUE THING IN A MARKET AND A MAN STOPPED COMING FOUR MORNINGS A WEEK.** She has not told him she is the reason and cannot ask him why, because one of the two answers is that market and she cannot rule it out. The woman he used to sit with has nobody there in the mornings now and has said nothing to anybody and is worse off than she was in February.
- **A MAN STOPPED ASKING FOR A CORNER OF A STRIP OF GROUND IN NOVEMBER AND A WOMAN WENT TO THE SAME DOOR THREE TIMES SINCE JANUARY ON HIS BEHALF AND DID NOT TELL HIM, AND ON THE SIXTH OF APRIL HE BOUGHT TWO MORE HIVES AND PUT THEM IN NINE FOOT BEHIND HIS OWN HOUSE.** He now has three where he should have two, in view of a back window he can see from where he stood them. She was told in January that nobody had ever asked about that ground, which was true, and she believed it, and he has never been told any of it.
- **A MAN DECIDED IN JANUARY NOT TO SAY ANYTHING ABOUT A HUNDRED YARD OF ROAD AND IS GOING TO KEEP WALKING PAST, AND A STRANGER HELD HIMSELF STILL ON THE FAR KERB IN MARCH AND LET A DOG BE OVER AND SAID NOTHING.** He watched the whole of it from his own kitchen and did not go down.
- **A MAN HAS SPENT FIVE WEEKS UNABLE TO GET FROM ONE READING OF THREE OPENINGS ON A TOP FLOOR TO THE OTHER, AND A SCAFFOLD CAME DOWN ON THE TWENTY-SECOND OF MARCH, AND HE STOOD WHERE THE LADDER HAD BEEN FOR ABOUT FOUR MINUTES AND COULD NOT SEE THEM FROM UNDERNEATH.** He does not knock on doors on that street and the reason is in 2013 and 2016 and is not that street.
- **A JOINER FOUND TWO SAWN MARKS UNDER NINE OF HIS OWN AND DID NOT SAY SO, AND A WOMAN FOUND OUT HER SON MADE THEM WHEN HE WAS SIX, AND NEITHER TOLD THE OTHER.** The frame went on a lorry on the twelfth of April with both marks on it.
- **A WOMAN CARRIED A STRANGER'S FUCHSIA IN OUT OF THE RAIN AND HAS TOLD HERSELF THE SMALL ACCOUNT, AND THE MAN WHO LEFT IT USED ON IT THE ONE SENTENCE HE USES ON EVERYTHING IN THAT HOUSE, WHICH HE HAS USED ON ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED THINGS.** He came back at four, looked at it for a second or so, and drove off with a bag in his own van.
- **A WOMAN DROVE A MAN FOUR MILES EVERY SUNDAY FOR TWENTY YEARS AND IN MARCH HE DROVE HIMSELF, AND SHE WENT BACK TO THAT CORNER ON THE MONDAY AND STOOD THERE TWELVE MINUTES WITH A BAG IN HER HAND, AND HE WAS FOUR MILES AWAY SITTING IN A ROOM WITH EIGHT OTHER MEN.** She gave him a true answer to a question he had not asked and is not going to correct it.
- **TWO MEN STOOD AT SIX FOOT OF WALL FOR AN HOUR AND NEITHER SAID THE THING, AND ONE OF THEM SAID OUT LOUD THAT NOTHING WAS GOING ON, AND BOTH HAVE DECIDED THE OTHER HAS STOPPED TRUSTING HIM AND NEITHER IS WRONG.** A woman behind them has watched the third man get in, four times since January, always at about that hour, and has told nobody.
- **TWO PEOPLE IN A HUNDRED-YARD WAY EACH WORKED OUT THAT THE OTHER SHOULD CUT FOUR YARD OF GREEN WOOD BACK, AND IT RAINED, AND HE ASKED WHETHER IT WAS OPEN, AND SHE SAID NO.** They stood in the rain about two minutes. Her lease is up in June and nobody in that row knows.
- **AND ON A WEDNESDAY EVENING NINE PEOPLE WERE ON A PARADE FOR THEIR OWN REASONS AND A VAN CAME BACK THE WRONG WAY AND PUSHED A CONCRETE PLANTER A FOOT OFF A WALL OUTSIDE A SHOP, AND NINE PEOPLE WALKED ROUND IT AND NOBODY STOPPED, AND THE DRIVER CAME BACK AND APOLOGISED AND THE WOMAN AT THE COUNTER SAID IT WOULD NOT GO, AND A MAN SHUT THE SHOP NEXT DOOR AS HE HAS FOR SEVEN YEARS AND NOBODY HAS ASKED HIM AND HE IS NOT THE MAN WHO PUTS SHUTTERS UP.**
- **AND IN A ROOM AT THE END OF A PASSAGE IN A BUILDING BY THAT WATER, A BOOK LIES OPEN WHERE SOMEBODY LEFT IT TO HOLD ITS OWN PAGE.** Nobody has added a twentieth line to it. **The gap under the last line had been there across one thousand five hundred and ninety-six days when the tenth of April came, and nobody counted it and nobody went down that passage.**

- **AND THIS BOROUGH STILL CARRIES FOUR ACCOUNTS OF ONE THING AND NOT TWO OF THEM WILL SIT BESIDE ONE ANOTHER.** No fifth has joined them, none has been withdrawn, no person has told another which of the four is theirs, and nobody on that parade set two of them next to each other or asked anybody what any of them was for. It is still a question, and nobody is putting it to anybody.

- **AND THERE IS STILL SOMETHING IN THIS BOROUGH THAT NOT ONE PERSON LIVING IN IT COULD NAME IF YOU STOPPED THEM IN THE STREET, AND IT IS THE SAME SOMETHING IT WAS AT THE END OF AUGUST, AND IT HAS NOT ALTERED BY SO MUCH AS A DAY, AND WHAT HAS HAPPENED TO IT SINCE IS THAT THERE IS NOBODY LEFT HERE WITH ANY REASON TO GO LOOKING FOR IT.**

- **AND THE BOROUGH IS ORDINARY AND THE FORTY-FIVE DAYS FROM THE TWENTY-SEVENTH OF FEBRUARY WERE THE SAME KIND OF DAYS AS THE ONES BEFORE THEM AND NOT A TURN OF ANYTHING.** A hedge closed over a way · a stranger walked a dog on the wrong side of a road · two sawn marks nobody spoke about · a plant carried in out of rain · a Sunday not driven · a scaffold came down · two men said nothing for an hour · a man stopped coming four mornings a week and a woman two hundred yard away watched him do it · a corner of ground went in March and nobody who wanted it knew · and a van went the wrong way and pushed a planter a foot off a wall and nine people walked round it.

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 514 — the Sunday of the twenty-fifth of August 2019, from about ten past seven in the morning until about half past one in the afternoon, in this city

- **THE TEN CHAPTERS AND WHAT THEY DID.** Ten chapters, 505 to 514, from Saturday the thirteenth of July 2019 to Sunday the twenty-fifth of August 2019. Every weekday was re-derived from the real 2019 calendar after the review of this block found four of them wrong, and the dates were not movedeal 2019 calendar, and the prompt printed the thirteenth of July as a Wednesday when it is a Saturday; the date was kept because it is 0504's own date plus three and the weekday was re-derived, and the prompt was not defended. One day-count appears in the prose, on its own chapter's own date, once, in the body, in digits: **1,733 at 0514.**

- **WHAT PEOPLE ARE CARRYING, AND NONE OF IT WAS RESOLVED.** A man stopped going down a hill and into the baths every morning in July and told nobody, and his wife found out from a towel that was not on a nail and cannot ask him why because the question would be her own admission that she has been counting the mornings; she has also worked out in a doorway that he had stopped going before his back went, and cannot put that either. A woman has put nine benches back where they belong every Sunday for twelve years and a man who goes in that garden has counted her rounds for years without ever speaking to her; he moved one of them two feet out of place on a Wednesday to see, she put it back without turning round, and each of them is carrying a belief about what the other knew that the other does not hold. Two brothers agreed in a yard in 2009 that one would go to their mother on a Sunday afternoon and the other in the evening, each took the evening believing the other had the afternoon, and their mother has had nobody in the afternoon for ten years; the younger found out in June, went to her house on a Monday in July and sat in her front room for three hours having decided not to do the one thing he could have done there, saw the flask and the cup that come out at half past six on a Sunday, and left without mentioning them, and has decided nothing. A man took a small bell off a wall in 1998 and told one person in 2003 and that person died in 2011, and a woman at his door in July asked him a question that was not about it and he answered her truthfully in about four seconds, and he has been carrying the speed of his own answer ever since. A woman has carried a can of water up three floors every morning for six years, a man living above her began taking the weight of it in July without a word, she carried on anyway for a fortnight, asked him why on the sixteenth, was told the can was heavy, and since the twenty-sixth has done it alone and is not going to ask. A man said a woman would be all right with it in a line of people in June and forgot it before he reached the front; she has carried it nine weeks because he looked at her face, he has carried it every morning because he thinks she kept it as an insult, and neither knows what the other is carrying. A man and a woman each spent twenty-five years being careful with each other over a blue umbrella in a corner in 1994; he asked a third person in May, was told she never thought it at all, has said nothing since and wishes he had not asked. Two men have two accounts of a ceiling coming down at two in the morning in May in two buildings four streets apart, a third man told the second version once and has passed it on twice, and on a Sunday in August he was told at a step that there is no lath in that building and apologised; the man who has been told nothing has said nothing. And a woman has been on a wall at six o'clock on a Tuesday twelve times since the middle of March without fishing, a man has asked her why twice and believed her twice, and on the Tuesday in August he told her he was not going the next one and asked her the wrong question, and she told him the truth.

- **THE BOROUGH HELD.** Ordinary time held and nothing in it turned. Eight steps between two streets have had a different rise on half of them since about 1974; about four people know it and all four found out by going over it. A man who is neither a tradesman nor a relation has been going to a house at number twenty-five on a Saturday morning for about four years and the woman there gave him a cup of tea the first time and has not given him one since and has never said she will not, and he has never asked and has never stopped coming. The four accounts of one thing are still in circulation, a fifth has not arrived, nobody has said which of the four is theirs, and on the twenty-fifth of August two men who each carry one put them side by side outside a place on a street and said *different, though* and went in. The line under the last entry of a working book in a side room off a way down by that water is still empty, and on that Sunday it had been empty for 1,733 days; a man went into that room that morning for a coil of cable, read the ruled line, put the book back the same way up and has told nobody. And the one thing this borough still cannot put a name to stands where it stood in the middle of August 2018; a woman got as far as the corner of her own street with her coat on at ten to one, stood for six minutes and went home.

## THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 524 — the Thursday of the seventeenth of October 2019, in this city

- **WHAT PEOPLE ARE CARRYING, AND NONE OF IT WAS RESOLVED.** A woman has kept a fixed six-minute walk at one end of a street for sixteen years and broke it once in June and told nobody, and a woman across from her who had been watching from her own front for five years has worked out that something changed and cannot ask, and has started standing in her own garden twenty minutes earlier on the mornings the other is coming back. A man carried another man's shopping across a row of bays on a Saturday for three years and stopped in August without a word, and the man he carried for found out four days late and has built a reason of his own that is not the first man's reason, and neither has said anything. Two women settled something in a queue in a town in 1997 and have both kept to their own reading of it for twenty-two years, and one was told in August that her reading was the honoured one and has decided nothing. A man said three words to a brother in a kitchen in 1996, and the brother's wife heard the same three words said to a third man in September, in the same voice and with the same hand, and put it to her husband on the twelfth, and was not denied. A woman has carried a felt hat to a hook in her own porch every Wednesday for seven years, and a man moved it once in May and never moved it back, and she went on taking it down for fifteen weeks and then stopped waiting, and one week later she put it there on the wrong evening because it was raining and a man walked past and stopped. Two people agree about every part of the eighth of May except one sentence spoken down a telephone, and neither has ever been wrong, and the third person they asked in September said she already knew and would not say which. Two men have described a third man neither has met for eight years and the two descriptions do not fit, and one was shown a picture in August and said his own description aloud anyway in front of the other, and the other went quiet and gave an account of his jaw. A woman and a man who work in the same building carry two accounts of one Wednesday in March and have never been able to check either, and in October they were in a room for nine minutes and she said a sentence and he said all right. A woman has written something down every day since the fourteenth of June and showed it to nobody until the twenty-second of September, and the man she lives with read a hundred and twenty days of it and has asked her nothing since, and has started writing the things he needs to say to her where she will find them.

- **THE BOROUGH HELD.** Ordinary time held and nothing in it turned. A heather behind number thirty-four has been spreading out of its border since June and the edging that would have held it has gone at two places. A swing at the end of a row has been making a noise since the summer and the oil that would stop it is in an outbuilding with a rusted lid on it. The line under the last entry of a working book in a room off a way down by that water is still empty, and on that Thursday it had been empty for 1,786 days; nobody went in, and it is the third week running in which nobody has gone in. The four accounts of one occurrence are still in circulation, a fifth has not arrived, nobody has said which of the four is theirs, and on that day the only thing that happened was five people standing in front of one another in a shop in the town and one of them saying something about the rain. And the one thing this borough still cannot put a name to stands where it stood in the middle of August last year; what has changed is not that it has been found but that the woman who was still going out is not going any more, and nobody saw her stop, and she has told nobody. The man in his sixties who was standing in that line went out that afternoon the way he does four or five times a year, got as far as the corner, and came home earlier than he goes, and he is the last one and nobody is going to ask him why.

- **WHAT PEOPLE ARE CARRYING, AND NONE OF IT WAS RESOLVED.** Two sisters did the same short walk together every morning for thirty years and a scarf went one way in the cold and came back the other; one of them stopped on a Thursday in September 2019 without a word to anybody about it, and the other went on going to the corner for seven weeks, and is not after a bus, and has lost the use of one arm for the scarf and has not considered stopping. On the twenty-fourth of October the one who stopped came out anyway and they were on the same pavement for sixty yards and neither of them lifted a hand, and at the corner four words were said about the wind. A man was given a true sentence in a yard in March and has carried it for seven months, and in October the man who said it handed the same sentence back to him at a building, and he had a question ready and did not ask it and has told nobody that he has got it. A woman has counted the people in a room every Monday since March 2000 with a hand down the side of her leg and has never said the number and has never written it down, and a man who came at the end of July said one sentence about it in September and was given one back, and on the fourth of November there was a woman in a green coat in that room who had not been in it before and the counter counted her in without asking and spent the whole interval deciding not to go over and did not go over. Two sisters have disagreed since 1999 about whether their mother was to be moved, and it is not about what anything costs and both reasons are good and neither has yielded, and on the fifth of November a woman who used to live next door and heard the younger sister say something in that house in 1999 in a room they did not know she was in told the older one what it was, and the older one has said nothing about it to anybody, and on the eighth she stood at the front of the house for about twenty minutes and did not go in. A man has said one word at an entrance every Wednesday for fourteen years and in October 2019 the word was not said, and a woman has come on the two Wednesdays since and told nobody, and on the thirteenth she stood at the end of a row in the rain for the best part of an hour, and he spent both of those evenings in his own front and told nobody.

The line under the last entry of a working book in a room off a way down by that water is still empty, and on that Wednesday it had been empty for 1,813 days; **nobody went in, nobody counted, and not one of these five chapters is in that room.** The four accounts of one occurrence are still in circulation, a fifth has not arrived, nobody has said which of the four is theirs, and nothing in these five chapters put any two of them beside each other, and the only thing that happened anywhere was five people standing in front of one another in a shop in the town. A woman has been putting a pair of spectacles back where she finds them since 2016, and since the Friday before the twenty-eighth of November a man in her house has been putting them back folded on a sill before she is up, and on the twenty-eighth she came down at twenty to six on purpose to catch him and he had already been down and gone back up, and each of them knows the other knows, and neither will be the one who says so.
